Tasks 3/4/5/6 are independent of each other; the planner may parallelize at PLAN-write time.
- **PLAN.md cadence:** standalone pre-Task-1 commit per the 04.3 (`c02eea7`) / 05.1 (`f23d08f`) standardized posture.
- **Verification gate:** §7.5's six gates with the substantive new requirement being all 5 affected Docker-gated fixtures (0003/0004/0005/0006/0008) GREEN simultaneously + the 3 unaffected (0001/0002/0007) staying green. Substantively closes phase-04.3 REVIEW C-1.
- **Carryforward closures projected at 05.4 state-6:** Phase-04.3 REVIEW C-1 — closed substantively at this sub-phase's state-4 verification commit. Phase-04.1 REVIEW M-claim — unblocked by the fixture-mask removal but stays deferred per the 04.3 disposition. No new I3-style or A-style closures.

### Phase-08 state-1 brainstorm

Brainstorm session decisions (this commit), all per the user's standing preference auto-memory `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") plus one explicit user-confirmed scope question on graceful-drain machinery:

- **Slug:** `08-admin-api-and-drain` — recommended natural-choice per the previous session's next-prompt; mirrors row-08 charter; preserves the `NN-<slug>` pattern.
- **Drain scope (the one explicit user-confirmed scope question):** **endpoint-triggered drain**. Adds `/drain_listeners` + `/healthcheck/fail` + `/healthcheck/ok` admin endpoints that trigger the EXISTING per-listener `DRAIN_BUDGET = 5s` drain machinery; wires `/server_info` + `/ready` to report `LIVE` / `DRAINING` state via a new shared `Arc<DrainState>`. Out of scope (deferred): `drain_time_s` config consumption, `Connection: close` injection on in-flight responses, full timed drain sequence. Rejected alternatives: full drain sequence (materially larger; likely forces split into 08.1/08.2 by surface scope); minimal report-only (drain fixture can't meaningfully assert behavior).
- **Structural approach:** option (a) — expand `crates/envoy-admin/` in place + a small shared `DrainState`. Rejected: dedicated `envoy-drain` crate (YAGNI for one type); generalized admin-router abstraction (premature for fixed 7-endpoint surface).
- **`DrainState` placement decided at SPEC time:** lives at `crates/envoy-listener/src/drain.rs`, re-exported from `envoy-admin::DrainState`. The natural `envoy-admin` placement creates a Cargo cycle (envoy-admin already depends on envoy-listener::ConnectionHandler); the resolution mirrors the M4 `DRAIN_BUDGET` hoist + the 05.3 (`envoy-http1` ↔ `envoy-http2`) + 07.1 (`envoy-filter` ↔ `envoy-http1`) cycle-resolution doctrine. **No new ADR required** — the resolution posture is doctrine-clear by precedent; the SPEC documents the decision directly per D-3.5 (an ADR is for novel decisions, not for applying an established pattern).
- **Endpoint surface (7 new endpoints):** `/server_info` (GET, JSON), `/clusters` (GET, text), `/listeners` (GET, text), `/config_dump` (GET, JSON), `/drain_listeners` (POST, empty), `/healthcheck/fail` (POST, empty), `/healthcheck/ok` (POST, empty). `/ready` + `/stats` + `/stats/prometheus` (06.1) retained. Method-strict per-endpoint allowlist; query-string honoring out-of-scope. `dispatch(method, path)` widens to a 3-variant enum (`Endpoint` / `NotFound` / `MethodNotAllowed { allow }`); closes 06.1 M1 as a side-effect.
- **06.1 carryforward closures (Task-1 preamble — D1/D2/D3):** REVIEW I2 (`serialize_response` case-insensitive header dedupe), REVIEW M1 (`reason_for_status` helper), REVIEW M4 (`DRAIN_BUDGET` consolidated to `envoy-listener`). All three named to phase 08 by the 06.1 + 06.2 + 06.3 + 07.2 "Phase-06.1 rollovers" subsections.
- **ADR projection:** **NO new ADRs projected to land.** Recommended posture: `serde_json` (already permitted) handles `/config_dump` + `/server_info` JSON serialization; `tokio::sync::Notify` (already used; part of `tokio`) handles drain notification; `AtomicU8` handles `DrainStage`. Two conditional ADR slots stay reserved-available: **ADR-0032** (split decision; most likely landing site at state 2 if §6.1 split-gate fires per SPEC §6.1 projection ~21-23 tasks / ~1500-1800 LoC), **ADR-0033** (foundations grant for `/config_dump` — recommended posture is no-grant; would land only if `prost-reflect` or similar surfaces as a materially-worse-than-foundation result at state 3).
- **`config_dump` shape:** static bootstrap config only. envoy-rust has no xDS yet (§9 family, post-08); xDS-derived `ConfigDump` entries (`ClustersConfigDump`, `ListenersConfigDump`, etc.) defer to the xDS family. envoy-rust emits one `BootstrapConfigDump` entry; Envoy emits that PLUS xDS entries; the latter land on `allowlist_envoy_only_keys` of the new harness `BodyRule::JsonShape`. The `Bootstrap` struct gains `Serialize` derives (mechanical, no new dep).
- **Fixtures (2 new):** `0014-admin-config-dump-server-info` (reuses fixture 0008's bootstrap shape — HCM + STRICT_DNS + 1 listener; admin scrape × 4 endpoints) + `0015-admin-drain-listeners` (reuses fixture 0007's bootstrap shape — HCM + direct_response; drain-sequence assertion with `pre_admin_actions` + `post_admin_assertions` harness extensions).
- **PLAN.md cadence:** **the state-2 PLAN-write session decides single-phase vs split** per the §6.1 split-gate evaluation. SPEC §6.1 projects ~21-23 tasks / ~1500-1800 LoC — at or near the split threshold. **Recommended split shape (if gate fires):** 08.1 — admin endpoint surface + carryforward closures + fixture 0014 + harness extensions (~1000 LoC; D1-D8 + D14 + D15 + D17.1 + partial D17.3 + partial D17.4); 08.2 — drain machinery + fixture 0015 + parent-08 close-out (~800 LoC; D9 + D10 + D11 + D12 + D13 + D16 + D17.2 + partial D17.3 + partial D17.4). Mirrors 06/07's foundation-then-integration split shape.
- **Phase-00 I3 deferral continues:** endpoint-triggered drain exercises drain via `POST /drain_listeners`, NOT via signal. SIGKILL→SIGTERM of the harness subject subprocess stays deferred; `nix` crate still off the permitted-foundations list; phase 08 does not engage signal-based termination.
- **Verification gate:** `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §7.5's six gates with the substantive new requirement being all **15 Docker-gated fixtures (0001-0015) GREEN simultaneously** at the state-4 CI run (extending the 07.2 baseline of 13 with 0014 + 0015). h2spec ≥95% gate continues to hold at parent-05 baseline (no H2-framing surfaces engaged).
- **Carryforward closures projected at phase-08 state-6:** 06.1 REVIEW I2 + M1 + M4 — closed at Task-1 preamble (D1/D2/D3). No new I3-style or A-style closures projected; phase 08 lands clean.

### Phase-08 state-2 split

Split-commit decisions (this commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"):

- **Split-gate evaluation:** the parent-08 SPEC §6.1 projected ~21-23 tasks / ~1500-1800 LoC at SPEC-time. Re-evaluated at this state-2 commit: the **LoC dimension fires** (1500-1800 baseline vs ~1500 cap = 0-20% over before drift; with the parent-04.3 / parent-05.3 / parent-06.x / parent-07.x ~+20% drift adjustment, projection lands at 1800-2160 LoC, decisively over the cap). The task-count dimension is under (21-23 vs ~25 cap), but `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.1 says EITHER dimension fires the gate; the LoC dimension is sufficient. Split decision: **fire**.
- **Split shape:** **08.1 + 08.2 per the parent-08 SPEC §6.1 recommended shape**, taken directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. No alternative-shape brainstorm. 08.1 = admin endpoint surface foundation slice + 06.1 carryforward closures + fixture 0014 (~1080-1180 LoC / ~12-14 tasks; ~25-30% LoC headroom under the §6.1 gate). 08.2 = drain-machinery slice + fixture 0015 + parent-08 close-out + MVP-trunk close (~810-920 LoC / ~10-13 tasks; ~40% LoC headroom). Boundary by deliverable cohesion (endpoint surface vs drain machinery), NOT by traffic direction (which doesn't apply to admin) or by code area (the surfaces are architecturally separable per ADR-0032 §Rationale). Mirrors phase-02 ADR-0013 / phase-03 ADR-0017 / phase-07 ADR-0030 2-way precedents (this is the project's fourth 2-way split; phases 04 / 05 / 06 were 3-way).
- **Sub-phase ordering invariant: 08.1 → 08.2 cannot be parallelized.** Four enumerated reasons (per ADR-0032 §Rationale + 08.2 SPEC §5.1):
  1. 08.2's D9/D10 add new `AdminEndpoint` variants consuming 08.1's `Dispatch` enum (D4).
  2. 08.2's D5e patches the value source on 08.1's `ServerInfoBody` struct (the `state` field literal `"LIVE"` swaps to `match drain.current() { ... }`).
  3. 08.2's D13b widens 08.1's `AdminHandler::new` signature from the 5-arg shape to 6-arg by adding `Arc<DrainState>`.
  4. 08.2 closes the parent-08 ROADMAP row + the MVP trunk; cannot be closed before 08.1 is done.
- **Closing-sub-phase invariant: 08.2 owns parent-08 close-out + MVP-trunk close narrative.** Mirrors 02.2 → parent-02, 03.2 → parent-03, 04.3 → parent-04, 05.3 → parent-05, 06.3 → parent-06, 07.2 → parent-07. 08.1's state-6 commit only flips its own row `08.1` `in-progress` → `done`; the parent row `08` stays `in-progress` until 08.2's state-6.
- **STATE-advance posture: state 2 directly (skipping 08.1 state 1 brainstorm).** Per the established cross-phase precedent (parent-07 state-2 split commit `6db5a01`; parent-06 state-1+state-2 combined-recovery commit `1f7661a`), the parent state-2 split commit lands each sub-phase's SPEC AND advances STATE.md to the first sub-phase's state 2 directly. 08.1's SPEC is a surface-slice carve-out of the parent-08 SPEC (which already brainstormed the full surface); no separate 08.1 brainstorming session is needed. 08.2's SPEC is similarly a carve-out; per 08.2 SPEC §8, the session entering 08.2 may also route directly to state 2 if STATE.md says state 2, or to state 1 if STATE.md says state 1 — the 08.1 state-6 close-out commit (several sessions out) picks the disposition.
- **ADR ledger advance: ADR-0031 → ADR-0032.** ADR-0032 documents the split decision; lands at this commit per the parent-08 SPEC §7 pre-projected conditional-ADR-0032 slot. The pre-projected conditional ADR-0033 slot (foundations grant for `/config_dump` proto-JSON shape) stays reserved-available for 08.1 state 3 / 08.2 state 3 execution-time landing; recommended posture per parent-08 SPEC §5.3 + 08.1 SPEC §7 + 08.2 SPEC §7 is **no foundations grant** — `serde_json` (D-3.2 permitted) + `tokio::sync::Notify` (already used) + `std::sync::atomic::AtomicU8` (std-lib) cover the new surfaces end-to-end.
- **Cross-sub-phase boundary commitments 08.1 makes that 08.2 will consume:**
  - **`/server_info` `state` field's structural shape** — 08.1 emits `state: &'static str = "LIVE"` (or `String = "LIVE"`; planner picks at 08.1 PLAN time); 08.2 swaps the value-binding source from the literal to `match drain.current() { Live | HealthcheckFailing => "LIVE", Draining => "DRAINING" }`. The struct shape (the field, its type, its position in the JSON output) does NOT change at the 08.1 → 08.2 boundary.
  - **`AdminHandler::new` 5-arg signature** — 08.1 widens from 06.1's `new(config, registry)` to `new(config, registry, bootstrap, cluster_manager, start_instant)`. 08.2's D13b extends to `new(config, registry, bootstrap, cluster_manager, start_instant, drain)`.
  - **`Dispatch` enum surface** — 08.1's D4 lands `Dispatch::{Endpoint, NotFound, MethodNotAllowed{allow}}` with each `AdminEndpoint` variant declaring its `const ALLOWED: &str = "GET"`. 08.2's D9/D10 add 3 new variants with `ALLOWED = "POST"`; no further refactor of `Dispatch`.
  - **`/ready` 06.1 semantics** — 08.1 leaves 06.1's `/ready` 200-LIVE-only path unchanged (no drain types available). 08.2's D-ready extends with 503 DRAINING (body `"DRAINING\n"`) when `DrainState::current() == Draining`.
  - **No new stats at 08.1** — all D14 stats (`server.live`, `server.state`, `listener_manager.total_listeners_active`) land at 08.2 with the DrainState integration. 08.1's existing 06.1 + 06.3 stats surface carries forward unchanged.
- **Fixtures inheritance baseline:** 08.1's state-4 evidence anchor extends the 07.2 13-fixture baseline (`0001-0013`) to 14 fixtures (`0001-0014`) all GREEN simultaneously at the Docker-gated CI level. 08.2's state-4 evidence anchor extends to 15 fixtures (`0001-0015`) all GREEN simultaneously — the MVP trunk's full regression baseline.
- **Phase-08 SPEC remains in-tree unedited** as the committed historical artifact (mirrors the phase-04 / phase-05 / phase-06 / phase-07 parent-SPEC posture). For execution purposes the parent SPEC is superseded by the two sub-phase SPECs; cross-references via §10's "Parent: docs/envoy-rust/phases/08-admin-api-and-drain/SPEC.md" preserve traceability per D-3.4 / D-3.5.
- **08.1 conditional ADR-0033 (foundations grant for `/config_dump`):** stays reserved-available; recommended posture per 08.1 SPEC §7 is no-grant. If 08.1 state 3 surfaces a materially-worse-than-foundation result for JSON serialization (e.g., `prost-reflect` need to reproduce Envoy's protobuf-JSON shape exactly), ADR-0033 lands at the surfacing task per D-3.5.

### Phase-08.1 state-2 PLAN-write

State-2 decisions (this commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"):

- **Split-gate re-evaluation outcome:** 08.1 SPEC §6.1 projected ~12-14 tasks / ~1080-1180 LoC. This PLAN materializes **14 tasks / ~1530 LoC** projected (production ~685, tests ~635, fixture/doc ~210). Task count (14) at the SPEC's projected upper end; well under the BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md §6.1 ~25-task gate. LoC projection sits ~+2% over the ~1500-LoC soft gate — concentrated entirely in test + fixture material; production code (~685 LoC) is comfortably under the SPEC's ~700 LoC implied production-side target. **Decision: accept the drift; do NOT nest-split.** Per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi) ("Not recommended: nested splits"), the gate would have to fire materially (>15% over) before a nested split is considered; ~+2% is well under. The 07.2 / 06.x precedent ratifies the accept-drift posture for test-heavy projections (07.2 SPEC ~1500 → PLAN ~1600; 06.1 SPEC ~1300 → PLAN ~2010; 06.2 SPEC ~1300 → PLAN ~1875; none nest-split). If execution-time drift inflates a single task past ~10 sub-steps, the in-execution release valve is per-step commit splitting recorded in PROGRESS — NOT a phase-level nest-split.

- **Task organization:** 14 tasks per the SPEC §6.3 recommended order with co-located D1+D2 (both at `serialize_response` site). Task 1 = D1+D2 (06.1 REVIEW I2 + M1); Task 2 = D3 (06.1 REVIEW M4 DRAIN_BUDGET hoist); Task 3 = D4 (Dispatch enum + dispatch refactor); Task 4 = Bootstrap Serialize derive cascade + roundtrip sanity check (D6 prereq; isolated to its own task per the mechanical-risk-surface architecture lock-in); Task 5 = D13a (AdminHandler::new widening + envoy-bin wiring + `format_iso8601` pub wrapper); Task 6 = D6 (`/config_dump` — D6 before D5 per SPEC §6.3); Task 7 = D5 (`/server_info`); Task 8 = ClusterManager::clusters() accessor + D7 (`/clusters`); Task 9 = D8 (`/listeners`); Task 10 = D15 (`BodyRule::JsonShape` + `BodyRule::TextLines` harness rules); Task 11 = D17.1 (fixture 0014 + Docker-gated wrapper); Task 12 = D17.3a (fuzz corpus seed); Task 13 = D17.4a (in-process backstop); Task 14 = state-4 verification + STATE-advance.

- **20 architecture-decision lock-ins** per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Key non-obvious choices:
  - **D6 (`/config_dump`) before D5 (`/server_info`)** — D6 leading reveals YAML-vs-JSON-roundtrip surprises in the `Bootstrap` Serialize cascade before they compound (SPEC §6.3).
  - **`Bootstrap` Serialize cascade is its own dedicated task (Task 4)** before D6 — isolates the mechanical-risk surface.
  - **`/server_info.state` is hardcoded `"LIVE"` as a constant** at 08.1 (SPEC §5.4); 08.2's D5e patches the value-binding source; struct shape unchanged at the boundary.
  - **`/server_info.command_line_options` is built once at construction time** as a `BTreeMap<String, serde_yaml::Value>` field on `AdminHandler` (widens the constructor to 6-arg, NOT 5-arg as SPEC §3 D13a phrased it; recording as PLAN-time deviation #1).
  - **`ConfigDumpBody` uses borrowed-reference shape** (`bootstrap: &'a envoy_config::Bootstrap`) — avoids needing a `Clone` cascade on `Bootstrap` and its subtypes. Closes the SPEC §5.3 implicit Clone concern.
  - **`format_iso8601` reuse** — add a `pub fn format_iso8601(t: SystemTime) -> String` wrapper at `envoy-accesslog::lib.rs` (the internal `pub(crate)` impl stays internal); add `envoy-accesslog` + `envoy-cluster` as path-deps of `envoy-admin`.
  - **`ClusterManager::clusters()` accessor lands as Task 8 prerequisite** — ~5-10 LoC; the SPEC §6.5 anticipated this gap.
  - **`/clusters` minimum-line emission** — 08.1 ships only the two structural lines per cluster (`<name>::observability_name::<name>` + `<name>::default_priority::endpoints`); per-endpoint numeric-counter lines are absent; the harness `BodyRule::TextLines` `allowlist_envoy_only_lines` covers the Envoy-side counter line set on fixture 0014.
  - **`/listeners` sorted-by-name** for deterministic output.
  - **No new ADRs expected.** Ledger head stays ADR-0032. ADR-0033 reserved-available for execution-time landing only if reality forces it (per SPEC §7 conditional foundations grant for `/config_dump` proto-JSON shape — recommended posture is no grant).
  - **PROGRESS.md skeleton + Task 1 preamble land alongside PLAN.md at state-2** (07.2 / 06.2 cadence; divergence from 07.1's "PROGRESS created at Task 1" pattern).

- **6 PLAN-write SPEC corrections** (each verified against HEAD `56dee82`):
  1. DRAIN_BUDGET hoist target site is a module-level pub const, NOT the existing local-fn-scope const (both existing declarations get deleted; new one introduced).
  2. `format_iso8601` reuse requires visibility promotion (new `pub` wrapper at `envoy-accesslog::lib.rs`) + crate dep addition (`envoy-accesslog` path-dep on `envoy-admin`).
  3. `ClusterManager` does NOT currently expose a `.clusters()` accessor; Task 8 lands one as the D7 prerequisite.
  4. `AdminHandler::new` 2-arg current shape confirmed for D13a widening; the actual widened shape is 6-arg (not 5-arg as SPEC §3 D13a phrased) because `command_line_options` is threaded as a constructor field per architecture-decision lock-in #7 — PLAN-time deviation #1.
  5. `Bootstrap` and transitively-owned types currently derive `Debug+Deserialize` (sometimes with `PartialEq`/`Default`); NO `Serialize` derives anywhere. Cascade is ~25-32 derive-line edits; `ConfigDumpBody` uses borrowed-reference shape to avoid a Clone cascade.
  6. `AdminEndpoint::from_path` retained alongside new `dispatch` method (the dispatch refactor adds the method + the enum; `from_path` stays reachable for backward compatibility within the crate).

- **Carryforwards engaged in 08.1:** 06.1 REVIEW I2 (Task 1 D1), 06.1 REVIEW M1 (Task 1 D2 + structurally again at Task 3 D4), 06.1 REVIEW M4 (Task 2 D3). All three close at PLAN Task 1+2 (Task-1 preamble per SPEC §6.2).

- **Standing inventory carryforwards inherited but NOT engaged in 08.1** (carry forward unchanged unless coincidentally engaged):
  - 06.3 REVIEW I1 — structurally closed at 07.1 via two-layer attestation pattern; 08.1 continues per-task PROGRESS test-bucket attestation + state-4 Docker-gated CI anchor.
  - 06.3 REVIEW I2 — upstream-robustness family is the named owner; not engaged.
  - 06.2 REVIEW M1/M2/M4/M5, 06.1 REVIEW M2/M3/M5/M6, 05.3 REVIEW I2, 05.2 REVIEW I1/I2/I3, 04.1 REVIEW M5/M9/M-claim/M1/M2/M4/M7, 02.2 REVIEW M1 — all carry forward indefinitely; not engaged.

- **Cargo.lock cadence:** the phase-04.1 REVIEW M5/M9 ratification ADR carries forward unchanged through 08.1 — no new top-level Cargo deps; Task 5 + 6 add workspace-internal path-deps only (`envoy-accesslog`, `envoy-cluster`) and `serde_yaml` as a `[dependencies]` line on `envoy-admin` (already used at other crates in the workspace; not a new top-level dep).

- **Phase-00 I3 deferral continues:** 08.1 is a docs-and-endpoint surface; drain mechanism (08.2) exercises drain via `POST /drain_listeners`, NOT via signal. 08.1's fixture 0014 does NOT need graceful subprocess termination.

- **No ADR landed at this state-2 commit.** DECISIONS.md ledger head stays **ADR-0032** (per the recommended no-foundations-grant posture per 08.1 SPEC §7). ADR-0033 stays reserved-available for the 08.1 state-3 execution arc if reality surfaces a materially-worse-than-foundation result for JSON serialization.

- **BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md unchanged at this commit.** The new "Admin endpoint body shapes" subsection + the 4 GET-endpoint rows + the header allow-list dedupe note land at the specific task commits where each is first empirically exercised (the 07.2 / 06.x doctrine — contract extensions land at empirical-engagement task time, NOT at PLAN-write time).

### Phase-08.1 rollovers (from REVIEW.md §1-§8)

The 08.1 REVIEW (reviewed range `7dbd984..03e6435` inclusive of the `0e2987d` Task-14 SHA-fixup; landed at the state-5 commit `7c5adf2`) concluded **Approved with M-track follow-ups** — **zero Critical**, **zero Important**, **4 Minor findings (M1-M4)**. Severity calibration matches the parent-06 / 07.1 / 07.2 sub-phase cadence (06.1 / 06.2 / 06.3 / 07.1 / 07.2 all landed Approved-with-M-track). Per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §5.2, the phase did NOT re-enter state 3 — no Critical or Important findings — and the verdict routed cleanly to the state-6 close-out (this commit). The §7 per-task code-quality reviewer findings across Tasks 1-13: 2 tasks (4, 11) carried one post-substantive amend each before SHA-fixup; 11 tasks (1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 13) landed with only accepted-as-noted Minors. Task 14's surface (1 fixture-allowlist line addition + docs) was uncontroversial; no separate code-quality review required at the state-4 commit per the 07.2 Task 10 / 06.1 Task 14 / 06.3 Task 12 state-4-commit precedent. No per-task finding was propose-cleanup-in-08.1 — the state-4 evidence anchor at `03e6435` / CI run `25964680619` is complete.

Items closed in 08.1 (all 3 carryforwards from the 06.1 REVIEW — confirmed by the holistic state-5 review):

- **06.1 REVIEW I2** (`serialize_response` injects 5 standard headers unconditionally — duplicate-header hazard if a future endpoint sets the same name) — **CLOSED at Task 1 (`d43d97a`, D1).** `crates/envoy-admin/src/handler.rs::serialize_response` rewrote the default-header emission block with case-insensitive dedupe via a `has_header` closure calling `eq_ignore_ascii_case`. The 4 standard admin headers (`cache-control`, `x-content-type-options`, `server`, `date`) are now dedupe-guarded; the always-emitted `connection: close` line is intentionally OUTSIDE the dedupe set per the 06.1 no-keep-alive posture. 7 new unit tests in `serialize_response_dedupe_and_reason_tests` verify the dedupe is case-insensitive AND that caller-provided headers override. The chain 06.1 → 06.2 → 06.3 → 07.1 → 07.2 → 08.1 ends at this commit.
- **06.1 REVIEW M1** (`resp.reason.unwrap_or("OK")` at `serialize_response` — every status code reports "OK" as the reason phrase if `reason` is `None`) — **CLOSED at Task 1 (`d43d97a`, D2)** + **structurally reinforced at Task 3 (`7188e7f`, D4).** `reason_for_status(u16) -> &'static str` helper landed at module level in `crates/envoy-admin/src/handler.rs` covering 200/400/404/405/500/503 + fallback "OK"; `serialize_response`'s status-line construction now uses `resp.reason.unwrap_or_else(|| reason_for_status(resp.status))`. The Task 3 `Dispatch::MethodNotAllowed { allow }` arm structurally reinforces by having every endpoint declare its 405-method-allowlist surface via `allowed_method(&self) -> &'static str` — the 405 reason phrase is no longer hand-rolled per-endpoint. The chain 06.1 → 06.2 → 06.3 → 07.1 → 07.2 → 08.1 ends at this commit.
- **06.1 REVIEW M4** (`DRAIN_BUDGET` duplicated across two source files — `crates/envoy-admin/src/handler.rs` module-level + `crates/envoy-listener/src/lib.rs` local-fn-scope inside `Listener::serve`) — **CLOSED at Task 2 (`c6368f4`, D3).** A single `pub const DRAIN_BUDGET: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);` at module level in `crates/envoy-listener/src/lib.rs`; both prior declarations deleted; `crates/envoy-admin` re-imports via `use envoy_listener::DRAIN_BUDGET;`. 3 new lockstep tests landed. The chain 06.1 → 06.2 → 06.3 → 07.1 → 07.2 → 08.1 ends at this commit.

Items carrying forward (the 4 Minor findings — all awareness-only / close-opportunistically; **none has a hard-gated owner phase; none gated the 08.1 state-6 close-out**):

- **M1 (Minor; fixture 0014 README contradicts disk reality on the in-process backstop).** `tests/fixtures/0014-admin-config-dump-server-info/README.md` lines 118-121 claimed *"No in-process backstop exists for this fixture"* — correct at Task 11 (`6ee1ad7`) when the README was authored, but false at HEAD because Task 13 (`fcfb940`) landed exactly that backstop at `crates/envoy-bin/tests/admin_config_dump_server_info.rs` (230 LoC). The README was not updated in lockstep. **Why it matters:** D-3.4 stranger-readability — a future maintainer hitting a failure at the in-process level might double back to the README, find the "no in-process backstop exists" claim, and waste time hunting for an inconsistency. **CLOSED at THIS state-6 close-out commit** per REVIEW.md §4 disposition: the "Acceptance signal:" paragraph at lines 118-121 is replaced with text naming both attestation layers (Docker-gated differential + in-process happy-path) with their roles, and cross-references Task 13's narrative for the trivial-echo-filter workaround. Metadata-only fix; no test re-run required. The chain ends.
- **M2 (Minor; `value_may_differ_keys` field on `BodyRule::JsonShape` has no inline field-level doc-comment).** `tests/differential/src/lib.rs:300` declares `pub value_may_differ_keys: Vec<String>`; the variant-level doc explains the semantics correctly and the dispatch arm implements it correctly, but a one-line doc-comment on the field itself would aid scanability when reading the struct declaration in isolation (e.g., from an LSP hover). **Fix sketch:** prepend `/// Shared keys whose values may differ bilaterally; presence is required, value equality is not.` above line 300. **Disposition: close opportunistically at 08.2 D16** when `Driver::AdminScrape` is widened with `post_admin_assertions`. The same edit pass naturally touches the surrounding rule declarations. ~1-line edit. Named owner: 08.2.
- **M3 (Minor; `command_line_options: BTreeMap<String, serde_yaml::Value>` cloned per-request inside `/server_info` rendering).** `crates/envoy-admin/src/handler.rs` clones the `BTreeMap` from `&self` into the borrowed `ServerInfoBody<'a>` on every `/server_info` GET. At 08.1's scope the map has a single entry (`config_path`) so the clone cost is negligible — the construction-site comment explicitly notes "small in 08.1 — single `config_path` entry". **Why it matters:** purely forward-looking — if a later phase widens the CLI surface (more flags, more options, runtime-RTDS-fed entries) the per-request clone scales linearly with the map size. **Fix sketch (if ever needed):** wrap the map in `Arc<BTreeMap<...>>` at construction time and clone the `Arc` instead. ~5 LoC; mechanical. **Disposition: carry forward indefinitely** — activates only if a future deployment surfaces a wide CLI option set. Named owner: whichever future phase first widens `envoy-bin`'s CLI surface beyond the current single-flag (`-c <config>`) shape.
- **M4 (Minor; `walk_pointer` produces opaque error on dotted path with an empty segment).** `tests/differential/src/lib.rs:379-394` walks a dotted-segment path like `"node..id"` (double dot) by `dotted_path.split('.')` — the empty segment between the two dots becomes the literal empty string `""`, which `parse::<usize>()` then fails on, then `cur.get("")` fails on the object, and the error message becomes `"key not found: "` (trailing blank). Unreachable in normal use (every dotted-path subtree assertion in fixture 0014's `expectations.yaml` is well-formed). **Fix sketch:** add a one-line guard at the top of `walk_pointer` body: `if dotted_path.split('.').any(|s| s.is_empty()) { anyhow::bail!("dotted path contains empty segment: {dotted_path:?}"); }`. **Disposition: close opportunistically at 08.2 D16 or whichever later phase first extends `walk_pointer` / `JsonShape`.** ~3-line guard. Named owner: 08.2 or later harness-touch phase.

Process note (carried forward to 08.2 per REVIEW.md §4): Task 13 deviation #5 surfaced a real schema-vs-runtime inconsistency — `envoy-config` parser accepts `filter_chains: []` (no validator rejects empty filter chains at this layer), but `envoy-bin/src/main.rs:158` panics with `.expect("validator guarantees ≥1 filter")` when no filter is present. The Task 13 PROGRESS narrates this clearly and the workaround (add a trivial `envoy.filters.network.echo` filter) is correct for an admin-only backstop, but this is effectively a **schema-constraint drift item** that 08.2's `admin_drain_listeners.rs` in-process backstop will face symmetrically. The 08.2 PLAN-writer should EITHER (a) add a parse-time validator to `envoy-config` rejecting empty `filter_chains` (closes the inconsistency at the schema layer; small validator extension), OR (b) document the trivial-filter workaround inline in the 08.2 backstop's bootstrap construction and move on (matches the Task 13 disposition; zero schema-layer work). Recommended: option (b) at 08.2 state-3 + option (a) deferred to a hardening phase. **Do not re-open Task 13** — its disposition is correct.

Task 1 = D11 DrainState foundation; Task 2 = D14 gauges (server.live + server.state + listener_manager.total_listeners_active); Task 3 = D9 + D10 three POST endpoints (mechanically symmetric — one task per architecture-decision lock-in #8); Task 4 = D13b AdminHandler::new 7-arg widen + envoy-bin DrainState construction; Task 5 = D5e /server_info state-source rebind + D-ready /ready drain-aware response (combined per lock-in #9); Task 6 = D12 Listener::serve 2-arg widening + listener_manager RAII guard + signature-update churn; Task 7 = D16 Driver::AdminScrape pre_admin_actions + post_admin_assertions + 08.1 REVIEW M2 + M4 closures (folded per lock-ins #21 + #22); Task 8 = D17.2 fixture 0015 + Docker-gated wrapper + BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT Admin-action effect equivalence subsection; Task 9 = D17.3b fuzz corpus seed (independent); Task 10 = D17.4b in-process backstop (HCM + direct_response shape per Task 10 architecture deviation #1 — NOT the trivial-echo-filter workaround); Task 11 = state-4 verification + STATE-advance to state-5-next.

- **30 architecture-decision lock-ins** per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Full table in `PLAN.md` "Architecture decisions locked at PLAN-write time" section. Key non-obvious choices:
  - **D11 DrainState placement** at `crates/envoy-listener/src/drain.rs` with `envoy-admin::lib.rs` re-export per parent-08 SPEC §5.1's Cargo-cycle resolution (mirrors M4 DRAIN_BUDGET hoist + 05.3 / 07.1 cycle-resolution doctrine; no new ADR).
  - **DrainState::new(&Arc<StatsRegistry>)** registers `server.live` + `server.state` gauges at construction and stores both as `Arc<Gauge>` fields per SPEC §6.4 "the simpler DrainState field shape is recommended" — one source of truth; no polling task; no separate gauge-handle threading.
  - **DrainStage `#[repr(u8)]`** discriminant load-bearing — directly emitted as `server.state` gauge value (Live=0, HealthcheckFailing=1, Draining=2).
  - **`drain()` notify-once semantic** via `compare_exchange` with `Ordering::AcqRel` + `notify.notify_waiters()` called ONLY on CAS-success in the `Live | HealthcheckFailing → Draining` arms; repeat `drain()` calls fail the CAS silently and do NOT re-notify.
  - **`drain_signal()` shape:** `Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = ()> + Send + '_>>` with already-Draining early-return via `Box::pin(std::future::ready(()))`. Avoids unsafe pin-projection (crate `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]`). Heap allocation per call acceptable (~once per listener-lifetime per drain event).
  - **`/server_info.state` value-binding swap** at Task 5 — pure source-rebind from literal `"LIVE"` to `match handler.drain().current() { Live | HealthcheckFailing => "LIVE", Draining => "DRAINING" }`; `ServerInfoBody<'a>.state: &'static str` shape unchanged at the 08.1 → 08.2 boundary.
  - **`/ready` drain-aware body strings:** Live → 200 LIVE (body `"LIVE\n"`); HealthcheckFailing → 503 Service Unavailable (body `"Service Unavailable\n"`); Draining → 503 Service Unavailable (body `"DRAINING\n"`) per SPEC §3 D-ready + fixture 0015 sub-case 3.
  - **`listener_manager.total_listeners_active` gauge scope:** counts only listeners going through `envoy_listener::Listener::bind`/`serve` (HCM + tcp_proxy paths). Echo path + admin path use `TcpListener::bind` + custom serve fns and are naturally excluded per parent-08 SPEC §5.5. Idempotent re-registration in `Listener::bind` via the existing registry parameter; RAII guard at `Listener::serve` entry/exit mirrors the 06.3 ConnGaugeGuard pattern.
  - **`Listener::serve` 2-arg widening** with the new `_ = drain.drain_signal() => { ... }` arm identical to the existing shutdown arm except for the log message.
  - **D13b AdminHandler::new widening from 6-arg to 7-arg** (adds trailing `drain: Arc<DrainState>` parameter); mirrors how 08.1's Task 5 added `command_line_options` last.
  - **D16 Driver::AdminScrape field shape:** `{pre_admin_actions, pre_requests, scrapes, post_admin_assertions}` with `#[serde(default)]` on the new fields for backward compat with 08.1-landed fixtures 0011 + 0014.
  - **D16 dispatch temporal sequence:** `pre_requests → pre_admin_actions → scrapes → post_admin_assertions` (NOT the inverse; corrected at Task 8 PLAN-write per the natural shape "verify pre-drain baseline → drain → verify post-drain state → wire-level assertion").
  - **AdminAction + AdminAssertion enum shapes:** `#[serde(tag = "kind")]` internally-tagged; `within_ms: u64` (NOT humantime; avoids a new top-level Cargo dep).
  - **DataPlaneConnectionRefused assertion semantics:** poll in 100ms intervals up to `within_ms`; success on ECONNREFUSED OR immediate-EOF; either disposition accepted per SPEC §3 D16.

Per the 06.x / 07.x / 08.1 doctrine — contract extensions land at the task where each is first wired or empirically exercised.

Named owner: future hardening or filter-family phase that extends the select. Non-blocking.
- **M2 (Minor; `Err(0)` sentinel in `drain()`'s second-CAS-skip branch collides semantically with `DrainStage::Live as u8` discriminant).** `crates/envoy-listener/src/drain.rs:204` reads `Err(0) // Sentinel — we already succeeded via from_live.`. The `0` value happens to be the `DrainStage::Live` discriminant; functionally fine because only `.is_ok()` is checked at line 206, but a reader could mis-read the encoding. **Fix sketch:** either (a) use `Err(u8::MAX)` as a sentinel that cannot collide + extend the comment, OR (b) refactor to a single boolean expression collapsing the conditional (~2-5 LoC). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches `drain.rs`. Named owner: future drain-time-config phase or feature-family extension. Non-blocking.
- **M3 (Minor; BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md "Admin-action effect equivalence" rows for `/healthcheck/fail` + `/healthcheck/ok` claim "100ms" wire-flip budget unenforced).** `docs/envoy-rust/BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md:163-164` carries `"Both proxies MUST flip /ready to 503 within 100ms"`. The 100ms budget is doctrinal — `fail_healthcheck()` / `ok_healthcheck()` are synchronous `compare_exchange` + gauge updates + nothing else (sub-microsecond on a non-loaded host), but no test or fixture measures the latency or enforces the bound. **Fix sketch:** either (a) downgrade the row prose to `"synchronously observable after the POST response completes"` (~2 LoC contract edit), OR (b) add a `MaxLatencyMs` assertion type to the harness + a fixture or test that bounds the flip latency (sizable; defer indefinitely). Recommendation: option (a). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT's "Admin-action effect equivalence" subsection. Named owner: future healthcheck-filter phase or operations-hardening phase. Non-blocking; doc-tightening only.
- **M4 (Minor; `empty_200_ok()` helper omits `content-type` entirely).** `crates/envoy-admin/src/endpoint.rs:570-577` constructs `Response { status: 200, reason: Some("OK"), headers: [("content-length", "0")], body: Bytes::new() }` — no `content-type` header. RFC 7231 §3.1.1.5 permits this for empty bodies; upstream Envoy v1.33's `/drain_listeners` empirically carries `content-type: text/plain`. Fixture 0015 does NOT assert on `/drain_listeners`'s `content-type` (the scrape is `/server_info`). **Fix sketch:** add `("content-type", "text/plain")` to the headers vec at `endpoint.rs:572` (~1 LoC). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at whichever future phase first adds a POST-endpoint response-header assertion fixture. Named owner: future POST-endpoint header-assertion phase. Non-blocking; upstream-parity polish only.
- **M5 (Minor; `drain_signal()` always heap-allocates via `Box::pin`).** `crates/envoy-listener/src/drain.rs:246, 248` returns `Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = ()> + Send + '_>>` — the dynamic-dispatch return shape is forced by the two-arm return type asymmetry (`std::future::ready(())` vs `Notified<'_>`). The `Box::pin` allocates on every `drain_signal()` call (once per listener-accept-loop iteration). Under MVP-scale fixture load the cost is trivial; under high QPS this would become a per-connection allocation. **Fix sketch:** convert to `pub async fn drain_signal(&self)` + an internal `enum DrainSignalFuture<'a> { Ready, Pending(Notified<'a>) }` implementing `Future` (~20-30 LoC; mechanical). **Disposition: carry forward indefinitely** — activates only if a future high-throughput deployment surfaces allocator pressure on the accept loop's drain-signal path. Named owner: whichever future phase first benchmarks the accept loop's allocator footprint. Non-blocking; forward-looking performance note only.
- **M6 (Minor; `admin_handler_new_6arg_tests::admin_handler_new_accepts_six_args_and_constructs` test name + module name now describe a 7-arg shape post-Task-4 widening).** `crates/envoy-admin/src/handler.rs:953-985` retains the 06.1-era test name + module name despite Task 4's 7-arg widening. **Why it matters:** D-3.4 stranger-readability — a future maintainer searching for "6arg" tests would find this misnamed surface. **Fix sketch:** rename module to `admin_handler_new_constructor_tests` + test to `admin_handler_new_accepts_arg_count_and_constructs` + trim the 70-line block comment to a 3-line note pointing at the sibling Task-4 coverage (~75-line edit). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches `crates/envoy-admin/src/handler.rs::tests`. Named owner: future admin-endpoint extension or test-organization cleanup pass. Non-blocking.
- **M7 (Minor; `drain_signal()` doc-comment "tens of microseconds" claim is unmeasured).** `crates/envoy-admin/src/endpoint.rs:531-535` (and a similar phrasing in `drain.rs` doc-comments) carries the claim that `notify_waiters → listener-serve-drop-listener` latency is "tens of microseconds" on Linux/macOS loopback. Structurally plausible but no benchmark or measurement is cited. **Fix sketch:** either (a) drop the quantification (`"sub-millisecond on a non-loaded host"`) OR (b) back with a `tokio::time::Instant` measurement in `serve_returns_when_drain_signal_fires`. Recommendation: option (a) (~1-line edit). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches `endpoint.rs::render_drain_listeners`. Named owner: future admin-action-surface extension phase. Non-blocking; doc-tightening only.
- **M8 (Minor; `AdminEndpoint::render`'s `Ready` arm at `endpoint.rs:163-168` is `unreachable!()`).** The registry-only `render` path 08.1 D6 introduced cannot serve handler-scoped endpoints; the `Ready` arm reads `unreachable!("Ready requires handler-scoped DrainState; dispatch via AdminEndpoint::render_with")`. The `render_with` dispatch at `endpoint.rs:194-200` correctly routes `Ready` through `render_ready_with(handler)`, so the `render` arm is genuinely unreachable in production. A future maintainer adding a registry-only dispatch site for `Ready` would hit a runtime panic rather than getting a compile error. The same concern applies to `ConfigDump` / `ServerInfo` / `Clusters` / `Listeners` arms (all handler-scoped + `unreachable!()` in `render`). **Fix sketch:** split `AdminEndpoint` into `enum AdminEndpoint { Registry(RegistryEndpoint), Handler(HandlerEndpoint) }` with separate render fns (~150 LoC; sizable typestate refactor). **Disposition: carry forward indefinitely** — activates only if a future feature requires registry-only dispatch of a currently-handler-scoped endpoint. Named owner: whichever future phase first introduces a synthetic-state admin endpoint requiring registry-only dispatch. Non-blocking; type-safety refactor opportunity only.
- **T1 (Minor; `serves_honors_shutdown_signal_with_drain_param` test at `crates/envoy-listener/src/lib.rs:~835` is a near-verbatim duplicate of `serves_honors_shutdown_signal`).** Task 6 code-quality reviewer flagged this as Important-deletion-opportunity; recorded here as Minor close-opportunistically per project doctrine (the existing `serves_honors_shutdown_signal` post-widening already exercises both arms). **Fix sketch:** delete the duplicate (~10 LoC). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches `crates/envoy-listener/src/lib.rs::tests`. Named owner: future hardening or filter-family phase. Non-blocking; test-suite polish only.
- **T2 (Minor; Task 5 negative-control test note — 2 LIVE-stage tests passed pre-impl by coincidence).** PROGRESS Task 5 deviation narrative narrates this honestly: `ready_returns_200_live_when_drain_state_is_live` + `server_info_state_is_live_when_drain_state_is_healthcheck_failing` (LIVE-stage half) passed pre-implementation because the existing 06.1 surface already returns 200 LIVE under default `DrainStage::Live`. The 3 stage-transition tests deterministically failed pre-impl. **Fix sketch:** add a `// negative-control: passes pre-impl as regression-equivalence` comment to the two tests (~4 LoC). **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches `endpoint::ready_drain_tests`. Named owner: future admin-endpoint extension phase. Non-blocking; TDD-audit-trail polish only.
- **T3 (Minor; Task 4 doc-comment forward-references at `handler.rs:151-158`).** The Task 4 `drain()` accessor doc-comment mentions Task 5 consumers (`render_server_info` patch, `render_ready_with`) that did not exist at Task 4 commit time. Now landed at Task 5; doc-comment is fully accurate post-arc. **Fix sketch: no fix needed — doc-comment IS accurate now.** Awareness-only doctrine-discipline note for future PROGRESS-narrative discipline. **Disposition: no action.**

### Phase-09 state-1 brainstorm

Brainstorm session decisions (THIS commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"). This is the **first post-MVP-trunk feature-family brainstorm session** per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 — the cadence-setter for all subsequent feature-family arcs. Mirrors the parent-NN state-1 brainstorm cadence (closest shape precedent: parent-08 state-1 brainstorm at commit `0202e38`):

- **Family pick: HTTP filters family.** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Alternatives scored across 5 dimensions (carryforward closure value / foundation pressure / architectural risk / contract-surface maturity / first-phase scope tractability):

  - **HTTP filters family (WINNER)** — closes 07.2 REVIEW M1 at named site; zero foundation pressure (hand-roll all filters per D-3.2); low architectural risk (extends 07.x framework); mature contract (Header allow-list + Stat-name mapping populated through MVP trunk); tractable scope (~900-1100 LoC for first filter).
  - Network filters family — no carryforward closure; introduces new network-layer filter chain framework (higher risk); first concrete network filter is larger than first HTTP filter.
  - Load balancing family — strong alternative; low risk (extends 02.x cluster LB seam); ~600-800 LoC for one LB algo (least_request); rejected at the family-pick because HTTP-filter-family's carryforward closure value (07.2 M1) is the load-bearing tiebreaker.
  - Upstream robustness family — closes 06.3 REVIEW I2 (synthetic 5xx backend) BUT scope is multiple subsystems (health checks / outlier detection / circuit breakers / retries / hedging / conn pooling); first phase tightly scoped to active HTTP health checks alone is ~1200-1400 LoC.
  - HTTP/3 + QUIC family — heavy foundation pressure (first `quinn` pull; h3spec gate); high architectural risk (new UDP listener path); LARGE multi-phase scope; rejected — too sprawling for the first post-MVP phase.
  - gRPC family — `tonic` already permitted but no concrete gRPC backend engaged yet; possible but unmotivated for the first.
  - xDS / dynamic config family — HIGHEST architectural risk (entire new state machine); largest by scope per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9; rejected for first.
  - Observability family — strong alternative; extends 06.x naturally; rejected at the family-pick for the same tiebreaker as Load balancing.
  - Runtime + hot restart family — hot restart HEAVY; rejected — too sprawling for first.
  - WASM host family — own multi-phase sub-project per §9; rejected — too sprawling for first.
  - Deprecated / edge features — not a substantive feature family (meta-bookkeeping only).

  Top-3 viable: HTTP filters > Load balancing > Observability. HTTP filters wins on carryforward closure value + smallest tractable first-phase scope + most mature contract surface. The 09 SPEC §1 + §3 record the picked family + first-filter scope.

- **First filter pick: `envoy.filters.http.local_ratelimit`.** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Per the §9 HTTP-filter sub-feature enumeration (header_mutation DONE at 07.2), considered 14 candidate filters along (config shape / foundations needed / framework extension needed):

  - **local_rate_limit (WINNER)** — filter-chain config; no per-route required; zero foundations (hand-roll token bucket via std-lib atomic + Mutex); zero framework extension (Decision::StopAndSend exists from 07.1).
  - cors — per-route CorsPolicy via typed_per_filter_config is primary; rejected — needs new per-route framework primitive.
  - fault — needs `rand` for percentage sampling (deterministic-only scope loses upstream parity); rejected — random sampling forces foundations grant.
  - buffer — tiny config (max_request_bytes); rejected — needs new body-buffering framework extension (larger than the filter itself).
  - csrf — per-route primary; same per-route extension as cors; rejected.
  - compression — needs `flate2`/`brotli`/`zstd` foundations grant + new pluggable-compressor primitive; rejected.
  - rbac — medium principal-matcher primitive; rejected (medium scope).
  - adaptive concurrency — sophisticated gradient-based algorithm; rejected (algorithm-heavy).
  - jwt_authn — needs `jsonwebtoken` foundations grant + JOSE primitive; rejected.
  - ext_authz / ext_proc — needs gRPC client + cross-process call protocol; rejected (depends on gRPC family).
  - oauth2 — needs HTTPS client + auth-state framework; rejected.
  - admission control — probabilistic + stats-heavy (P50/P99); rejected.
  - bandwidth limit — per-stream timing; rejected.
  - global rate limit — needs gRPC client + RLS protocol; rejected (depends on gRPC family).
  - lua / wasm — scripting hosts; own multi-phase sub-projects per §9; rejected.

  Top-3 viable: local_rate_limit > fault > buffer. **local_rate_limit wins** on: pure filter-chain config (no per-route extension); validates `Decision::StopAndSend(FilterResponse)` path with non-trivial 429 + `x-envoy-ratelimited` response shape (fixture 0013 / header_mutation only exercised `Decision::Continue`); 4-counter stat surface matching upstream Envoy v1.33 parity exactly; deterministic semantics (no `rand` foundations grant); no new top-level Cargo deps; ~900-1100 LoC including tests + fixture. The 09 SPEC §3 D3 records the hand-rolled token-bucket primitive design; SPEC §6.3 makes the concurrency torture test (`token_bucket_atomic_compare_exchange_under_concurrency`) REQUIRED per the 08.2 Task 1 fixup `fddabd2` TOCTOU-lesson precedent.

- **Phase id + slug pick:** **`09`** / **`09-http-filter-local-rate-limit`**. The id `09` is next sequential after `08.2` per the project's numbering convention (first post-MVP-trunk phase starts at `09` regardless of family). The slug mirrors `07.2-header-mutation-filter`'s hyphenation pattern — readable English (`http-filter-local-rate-limit`) over upstream-name verbatim (`http-filter-local-ratelimit`). The filter's typed_config `name:` field uses upstream's exact `envoy.filters.http.local_ratelimit` (no underscore between "rate" and "limit"); the slug + readable name + upstream config name are deliberately distinct surfaces per the 07.2 precedent.

- **depends-on pick:** **`07`** (the parent filter-chain framework). Per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 depends-on doctrine (HTTP-filter-family extensions name `07`). The 15-Docker-gated-fixture regression baseline established at parent-08 close carries forward per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §7.5 (b) (implicit, not in depends-on field).

- **Split-gate projection at state-2 (SPEC §6.1):** Single-phase, no split. Per SPEC §3 deliverable enumeration: ~10-13 tasks / ~900-1100 LoC (production ~430, tests ~530, fixture/doc ~150). Both dimensions comfortably under the §6.1 ~25-task / ~1500-LoC gate. Recommended posture: accept the empirical drift at PLAN-write if it materializes (~30-50% as observed at 06.x / 07.x / 08.x); do NOT nest-split per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi) + the established no-nest-split discipline across the parent-06 / parent-07 / parent-08 arcs.

- **ADR projection at state-1: NONE.** Recommended posture per SPEC §7 + §5.2 + §5.3 — no foundations grants required at any phase-09 lifecycle state; hand-roll token bucket per D-3.2's "Must be written from scratch" doctrine for filter engines. DECISIONS.md ledger head stays at **ADR-0032** through this state-1 commit. Two conditional ADR slots stay reserved-available:

  - **Conditional ADR-0033 — per-route filter config deferral.** Documents the project-wide deferral pattern for the HTTP-filter-family arc (filter-chain config only at the 09 baseline; per-route `typed_per_filter_config` defers to the future filter that first needs it; CORS is the natural close site). Recommended at state-2: defer ADR-0033 until a future filter actually needs per-route config (the deferral is doctrinally clear without an ADR). If the state-2 PLAN-writer disagrees, ADR-0033 lands at the state-2 PLAN-write commit. The ADR-0033 reservation cited in the 08.1 / 08.2 PLAN-write subsections (as a foundations-grant slot for /config_dump) was honored end-to-end at the 08.x execution arc with no grant landing; THIS posture re-purposes the ADR-0033 slot for the HTTP-filter-family per-route-deferral OR the phase-09 foundations-grant, whichever fires first.
  - **Conditional ADR-0034 — foundations grant.** No grant projected. If state-3 surfaces a materially-worse-than-foundation result for the token-bucket primitive (e.g., a need for `governor`, `ratelimit`, `parking_lot`, `humantime`, `humantime-serde`), ADR-0034 lands at the surfacing task. Recommended posture per SPEC §5.2: no grant. The std-lib `AtomicU64` + `Mutex<Instant>` + hand-rolled `Duration` parse (covering `"<N>s"` / `"<N>ms"` / `"<N>us"` shapes) is sufficient for the v1.33 documented TokenBucket surface.

  If both conditional ADRs land in lex-then-execution order, ledger advances `ADR-0032 → ADR-0033 → ADR-0034`. If only one lands, it takes ADR-0033. If neither lands, stays at ADR-0032.

- **Carryforward closure projection at state-1: 07.2 REVIEW M1.** The severed `position` plumbing (`_position: usize` parameter on `HttpFilterInstance::build` at `crates/envoy-filter/src/instance.rs:39` + the `.enumerate()` on `FilterPipeline::build_from_config` at `crates/envoy-filter/src/pipeline.rs:31`) is projected to close at SPEC §3 D5 per the 07.2 REVIEW M1 named-owner disposition ("close opportunistically — fold into whichever future HTTP-filter-family phase next adds a non-HeaderMutation `HttpFilterInstance` variant"). The hardcoded `position: 0` at `crates/envoy-filter/src/header_mutation.rs::map_entry`'s `FilterError::UnsupportedFilterType { position: 0, ... }` defense-in-depth construction is LEFT AS-IS per SPEC §3 D5 rationale (the position field is semantically slightly-wrong at that site but unreachable in normal operation; preserving it avoids touching `header_mutation.rs` in phase 09, which is doctrinally cleaner — minimum-touch the established 07.2 surface). The carryforward chain 07.2 → 09 ends at the D5-landing task.

- **No other carryforwards engaged in 09.** Standing inventory carries forward unchanged: 08.2 REVIEW M1-M8 + T1-T3 (none engaged by 09); 08.1 REVIEW M3 (forward-looking `Arc<BTreeMap<...>>` indefinite-carry; not engaged); 07.2 REVIEW M2 + M3 (Overwrite O(n²) YAGNI / fixture-0013 expected_body coupling; not engaged); 06.3 REVIEW I2 (synthetic 5xx backend; upstream-robustness family is named owner; not engaged); 06.2 REVIEW M1/M2/M4/M5; 06.1 REVIEW M2/M3/M5/M6; 05.3 REVIEW I2; 05.2 REVIEW I1/I2/I3; 04.1 REVIEW M5/M9/M-claim/M1/M2/M4/M7; 02.2 REVIEW M1; Phase-00 I3 — all carry forward indefinitely per their existing named-owner dispositions.

- **Fixture 0016 projection:** `tests/fixtures/0016-http-filter-local-rate-limit/` lands at SPEC §3 D8.1. Bootstrap shape mirrors fixture 0007's minimal HCM + `direct_response` data-plane surface so the bilateral assertion focuses on the filter, not on upstream complexity. Token bucket `max_tokens: 3, tokens_per_fill: 0, fill_interval: 60s` + 5-probe sequential probe list via `Driver::Http1ProbeList` (existing harness primitive from 04.2; no new harness extension required) → expected status sequence `[200, 200, 200, 429, 429]` (deterministic). Probes 4-5 carry `x-envoy-ratelimited: true` response header assertion. Fuzz corpus seed at `crates/envoy-config/fuzz/corpus/parse_bootstrap/hcm_local_rate_limit_filter.yaml` extends the corpus from 15 to 16 seeds (one per fixture). In-process backstop at `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_local_rate_limit.rs` mirrors `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_header_mutation.rs` shape.

Per the 06.x / 07.x / 08.x doctrine: contract extensions land at the task where each is first empirically exercised, NOT at PLAN-write time.

### Phase-09 state-2 PLAN-write

State-2 decisions (THIS commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"). Phase 09 IS the **first post-MVP-trunk feature-family state-2 PLAN-write**; THIS commit establishes the post-MVP cadence for all subsequent feature-family arcs. Mirrors the parent-08.2 state-2 PLAN-write commit `1aa250d` shape exactly (the most-recent state-2 PLAN-write precedent):

- **Split-gate verdict:** Single-phase, no split. SPEC §6.1 projected ~10-13 tasks / ~900-1100 LoC; THIS PLAN materializes **8 tasks / ~1295 LoC projected** (production ~380, tests ~655, fixture/doc ~260). Task count (8) below the SPEC's projected lower bound by design — the natural task granularity for the deliverable surface (D1+D2 co-located at Task 1; D3 split across Tasks 2 + 3; D4+D5 co-located at Task 4; D6 co-located with D3 at Task 3; D7.1 lands with D6 at Task 3; D7.2 lands with D8.1 at Task 5; D8.1 at Task 5; D8.2 at Task 6; D8.3 at Task 7; state-4 at Task 8). Both dimensions comfortably under `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.1 ~25-task / ~1500-LoC gate. **Decision: accept the projection; do NOT nest-split.** Accept up to ~+50% state-3 empirical drift per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi) + the established 06.1 (+55%) / 06.2 (+44%) / 06.3 (+46%) / 07.1 (+32%) / 07.2 (+7%) / 08.1 (+30%) / 08.2 (+45%) accept-drift discipline.

- **Task organization:** 8 tasks per PLAN §4 task summary. Task 1 = D1 envoy-config schema + D2 validator (co-located; ~325 LoC); Task 2 = D3 hand-rolled token bucket primitive + concurrency torture test per SPEC §6.3 (~220 LoC); Task 3 = D3 LocalRateLimitFilter runtime + D6 stats wiring + D7.1 4 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT Stat-name mapping rows per SPEC §6.5 cadence (~245 LoC); Task 4 = D4 HttpFilterInstance::LocalRateLimit variant + D5 07.2 REVIEW M1 closure co-located per SPEC §6.2 (~60 LoC); Task 5 = D8.1 fixture 0016 + Docker-gated wrapper + D7.2 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT `x-envoy-ratelimited` row per SPEC §6.5 cadence (~145 LoC); Task 6 = D8.2 fuzz corpus seed (~50 LoC); Task 7 = D8.3 in-process backstop (~170 LoC); Task 8 = state-4 phase-done verification + STATE-advance to state-5-next (~80 LoC docs). Dependency chain: 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 → 7 → 8 per PLAN §4 (Tasks 5/6/7 pairwise independent post-Task-4 but executed sequentially per the established 06.x / 07.x / 08.x cadence).

- **39 architecture-decision lock-ins** per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Full table in PLAN.md §2; grouped summary in PROGRESS.md Task 1 preamble. Key non-obvious choices:

  - **Token bucket primitive shape (#3-#9)** — `struct TokenBucketState { tokens: AtomicU64, last_fill_instant: Mutex<Instant> }`; lazy fill at decision time (no background refill task); single `compare_exchange` loop with `Ordering::AcqRel` semantics; `std::sync::Mutex` (NOT `tokio::sync::Mutex`) — sync framework iteration; partial-interval carry-forward for the `last_fill_instant` advance.
  - **REQUIRED concurrency torture test (#9)** per SPEC §6.3 — `token_bucket_concurrent_acquire_does_not_double_count`, 8 tokio tasks × 10_000 acquires; assert `total_true_returns == min(N*M, max_tokens)`. Mirrors the 08.2 Task 1 fixup `fddabd2` TOCTOU-lesson precedent (the project's most-Critical fix to date).
  - **Filter struct shape (#10)** — `Debug + Clone` derived; bucket caps + fill_interval on the filter (NOT on state) so `Clone` is cheap; bucket atomics live on the `Arc<TokenBucketState>` shared across all per-request pipeline clones.
  - **`decode_headers` shape (#11)** — synchronous (matches 07.1 framework); 4-counter increment discipline: `enabled` unconditional at function prologue; `ok` on `try_acquire` success before `Decision::Continue`; `rate_limited` + `enforced` on `try_acquire` failure before constructing the `Decision::StopAndSend(FilterResponse)`.
  - **429 synth response shape (#13)** — `FilterResponse { status: 429, reason: Some("Too Many Requests"), headers: [("x-envoy-ratelimited", "true"), ...response_headers_to_add], body: Bytes::new() }`. `content-length: 0` added by the H1/H2 codec writers; the filter does NOT add it.
  - **Schema struct renames (#20)** — `LocalRateLimitFilterConfig` → `LocalRateLimitConfig`; `HttpStatusCode` → `HttpStatus` (per existing `HeaderMutationConfig`/`RouterConfig` naming convention).
  - **`fill_interval` parse (#15)** — `serde_yaml::Value` on the struct; hand-rolled `parse_duration` covering `"<N>s"` / `"<N>ms"` / `"<N>us"` (the upstream Envoy v1.33 documented shapes). No `humantime-serde` foundations grant.
  - **HCM build site threading (#24)** — `Http1HCMConfig::from_config` at `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:185` extends the `FilterPipeline::build_from_config` call from `(&cfg.http_filters)` to `(&cfg.http_filters, &registry)`. **Single call site to widen** (NOT two; H2 reuses the same HCMConfig struct via re-export).
  - **D5 (07.2 M1 closure) ordering (#22-#23)** — co-located with D4 in Task 4 per SPEC §6.2 option (a). Hardcoded `position: 0` at `crates/envoy-filter/src/header_mutation.rs::map_entry` left AS-IS per SPEC §3 D5 rationale (minimum-touch the 07.2 surface).
  - **BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row landing cadence (#30 + #31)** — 4 stat-name mapping rows land at Task 3 (D6 stats-wiring) commit; 1 header allow-list row (`x-envoy-ratelimited`) lands at Task 5 (D8.1 fixture) commit. Per the 06.x / 07.x / 08.x doctrine — contract extensions land at the task where each is first empirically exercised, NOT at PLAN-write time.
  - **Fixture 0016 assertion strategy (#29 + #33)** — differential fixture uses `Http1HeaderRule::SetEqualModuloAllowList` (both proxies emit `x-envoy-ratelimited: true`; set-equal passes). **Direct per-header `x-envoy-ratelimited: true` value assertion lives at the in-process backstop (Task 7, D8.3), NOT at the differential fixture (Task 5, D8.1).** Mirrors the 07.2 fixture-0013 `x-filter-response-stamp: phase-07` pattern.
  - **No new ADRs expected (#34).** Ledger head stays ADR-0032. ADR-0033 reserved-available for execution-time landing only if reality forces it (per SPEC §7).
  - **PROGRESS.md skeleton + Task 1 preamble (#38)** land alongside PLAN.md at THIS state-2 commit per the 06.2 / 06.3 / 07.x / 08.x cadence (divergence from the 06.1 "PROGRESS created at Task 1" superseded pattern).
  - **`#![forbid(unsafe_code)]` (#35)** carries forward unchanged — no new crate roots in 09; new `crates/envoy-filter/src/local_rate_limit.rs` module inherits from the crate root attribute.
  - **Subagent-driven execution at state 3 (#37)** per `feedback_execution_style` standing preference; skip the two-option fork.

- **7 PLAN-write SPEC corrections** (each verified against HEAD `3025594` — the phase-09 state-1 brainstorm commit; full text in PROGRESS Task 1 preamble):

  1. `ConfigError` enum lives in `crates/envoy-config/src/lib.rs`, NOT `bootstrap.rs` as SPEC §3 D2 implies. Validator function `validate_http_filters` IS in `bootstrap.rs` (line 1597). Task 1 edits both files.
  2. The HCM filter-pipeline build site is the HCMConfig constructor (`Http1HCMConfig::from_config` at `envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:185`), NOT `serve_connection`/`handle_one_stream` as SPEC §3 D4 says. H2 reuses the same HCMConfig via re-export — single call site to widen.
  3. `Http1HeaderRule` is a unit-variant enum with only `SetEqualModuloAllowList`, NOT `Option<Vec<HeaderRule>>` as SPEC §3 D8.1 hedge implies. No harness extension needed; differential fixture uses set-equal-modulo-allow-list. Direct per-header assertion lives at the in-process backstop.
  4. `StatsRegistry::register_counter` takes `&self`, NOT `&Arc<Self>`. Threading `&Arc<StatsRegistry>` works via `Deref`.
  5. `HttpFilterInstance` carries 2 `#[cfg(feature = "test-util")]` variants (`TestStopAndSendOnDecode` + `TestStopAndSendOnEncode`) per 07.1 / 07.2; SPEC §3 D4 + D5 don't reference them. New `LocalRateLimit` variant goes between `HeaderMutation` and the test-util block; test-util variants preserved verbatim.
  6. `HeaderMutationFilter::build_from_config` is single-arg precedent. New `LocalRateLimitFilter::build_from_config` has two-arg shape (the registry is needed). Deliberate new precedent for any future filter that needs stats.
  7. SPEC §3 D1 names `LocalRateLimitFilterConfig` + `HttpStatusCode`; PLAN renames to `LocalRateLimitConfig` + `HttpStatus` for consistency with existing schema naming.

### Phase-09 state-3 execution arc + ADR-0033 corrective sequence + state-4 verification

State-3 execution arc landed in **7 substantive task commits + 4 ADR-0033 mid-execution corrective commits + 1 Task 6 follow-up + 1 state-4-reached docs-only commit = 13 commits** between the state-2 standalone-PLAN base `b9da8d4` and THIS Task 8 state-4-reached commit's new HEAD. Per `feedback_execution_style`, all 7 substantive tasks were dispatched to fresh subagents per `superpowers:subagent-driven-development` with two-stage review (spec compliance + code quality) per task.

**Tasks 1-4: foundation + framework integration (4 commits over the state-3 arc base `b9da8d4`).**

- **Task 1 (`818a3c5`)** — D1 envoy-config schema + D2 validator. Added `HttpFilterTypedConfig::LocalRateLimit` variant + 3 new schema structs (`LocalRateLimitConfig`, `TokenBucket`, `HttpStatus`) + 4 new `ConfigError` variants (`EmptyLocalRateLimitStatPrefix` / `TokenBucketMaxTokensMustBePositive` / `InvalidTokenBucketFillInterval` / `UnsupportedLocalRateLimitStatusCode`) + `validate_local_rate_limit_config` sub-validator + hand-rolled `parse_duration` helper + `default_status` helper + 16 unit tests in `local_rate_limit_tests` submodule. **Deviation #1 (the 8th SPEC correction discovered at task time):** PLAN Step 4 proposed new `HeaderValueOption` + `Header` structs that collided with the existing 07.2-landed `HeaderValueOption { header: HeaderValue, append_action: AppendAction }` (re-exported from `lib.rs:15`); resolved by reusing the existing 07.2 types per upstream-Envoy-canonical parity (`response_headers_to_add` field in `LocalRateLimitConfig` resolves to the existing `HeaderValueOption`). **Deviation #2:** non-exhaustive `match` in `crates/envoy-filter/src/instance.rs` once the new variant landed — resolved with a temporary bridge arm returning `FilterError::UnsupportedFilterType` for the LocalRateLimit case during the Tasks 1-3 interim window; Task 4 replaces with the proper dispatch.

- **Task 2 (`b5c81d2`)** — D3 hand-rolled `TokenBucketState` primitive at `crates/envoy-filter/src/local_rate_limit.rs` (new module). `AtomicU64` for live token count + `std::sync::Mutex<Instant>` for last-fill timestamp; lazy fill at `try_acquire` time via single `compare_exchange` loop with `Ordering::AcqRel`/`Acquire` semantics. **REQUIRED 8-thread × 10_000-acquire concurrency torture test** `token_bucket_concurrent_acquire_does_not_double_count` per SPEC §6.3 + PLAN lock-in #9 + the 08.2 Task 1 `fddabd2` TOCTOU-lesson precedent: spawns 8 tokio tasks each calling `try_acquire` 10_000 times; asserts `total_true_returns == min(N*M, max_tokens)` (1000 expected; 79K rejections); verifies post-state `tokens.load(Acquire) == 0`. Deterministic across 5 verification reruns. **Deviation #1:** Cargo.lock +2 lines (envoy-stats + tokio listed as edges of envoy-filter; refines PLAN lock-in #39's "empty diff" projection — path-dep additions DO produce metadata-only lockfile diffs). **Refinement:** clippy-driven `#[allow(dead_code)]` on struct + impl (Task 3 close site named in comments) + `manual_checked_ops` rewrite from `if`-based to `match checked_div(interval_nanos)` — both semantically equivalent to PLAN-verbatim shapes.

- **Task 3 (`70bad43`)** — D3 `LocalRateLimitFilter` runtime + D6 4-counter stats wiring + D7.1 4 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md Stat-name mapping rows. Filter struct wraps Task-2's `Arc<TokenBucketState>` + 4 `Arc<Counter>` handles registered via `StatsRegistry::register_counter` at `build_from_config` time. Stat namespace `http_local_rate_limit.<stat_prefix>.{enabled,ok,rate_limited,enforced}` matches upstream Envoy v1.33 parity. 6 new unit tests. **Deviation #1 (per Task 1 deviation #1):** test code adjusted to use existing 07.2 `HeaderValueOption { header: HeaderValue, append_action: AppendAction::AppendIfExistsOrAdd }` shape. **Deviation #2:** `envoy_config::parse_duration` visibility promoted `pub(crate)` → `pub` + re-exported from `envoy-config/src/lib.rs` (LocalRateLimitFilter's cross-crate call site needs it). **Deviation #4 (PLAN-text typo correction):** `FilterResponse.reason` is `Option<&'static str>`, not `Option<String>`; impl + tests use `Some("Too Many Requests")` directly.

Added the new variant between `HeaderMutation` and the `#[cfg(feature = "test-util")]` block; widened `HttpFilterInstance::build` signature `(hf, position: usize)` → `(hf, registry: &Arc<StatsRegistry>)` (drops `_position`; adds `registry`); widened `FilterPipeline::build_from_config` signature `(filters)` → `(filters, registry: &Arc<StatsRegistry>)` (drops `.enumerate()`); threaded `&registry` through `Http1HCMConfig::from_config` call site at `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:185`. H2 path reuses HCMConfig via re-export (no second call site). Updated 5 test sites for the cascading signature change. The Task-1 bridge arm in `instance.rs` REPLACED (not augmented). **Hardcoded `position: 0` at `crates/envoy-filter/src/header_mutation.rs::map_entry` PRESERVED per PLAN lock-in #23** — header_mutation.rs production code untouched (3 test-side cascade call-site updates only). **07.2 REVIEW M1 chain closes at THIS commit; the chain `07.2 → 09 ends`.** **Deviation #1:** narrower per-field `#[allow(dead_code)]` retained on `LocalRateLimitFilter::stat_prefix` (only read by the `#[cfg(test)]` accessor; production-build clippy flagged dead_code; PLAN Step 6 instructed removal of struct/impl-level annotations — the narrower per-field annotation is the minimal alternative).

**Mid-execution discovery + ADR-0033 corrective sequence (4 commits over Tasks 5-7).**

Task 5 dispatch (the Docker-gated differential fixture authoring step) surfaced 3 SPEC §2.2 + Task 3 lock-in #13 discrepancies against upstream Envoy v1.33's actual `envoy.filters.http.local_ratelimit` wire shape per empirical Docker observation:

1. **Upstream does NOT emit `x-envoy-ratelimited` from local_ratelimit** (that header belongs to the global ratelimit filter + router-side response-flag handling per upstream v1.33 source).
2. **Upstream's 429 body is the source-hardcoded `"local_rate_limited"` (18 bytes)**, not empty (the upstream proto has no configurable `response_body` field).
3. **envoy-rust's H1 HCM filter-StopAndSend writer-path skips standard-header decoration** — the synth-from-build paths (`synth_status`, `synth_direct_response`) populate the 5 standard HTTP/1.1 response headers inline; the filter-synth path (`RequestPath::SynthFromDecode` arm + encode-side `Decision::StopAndSend` arm) takes `filter_resp.headers` verbatim. A latent 07.x framework defect that 07.2 HeaderMutation never exposed (HeaderMutation's `decode_headers` returns `Decision::Continue` unconditionally — the filter only mutates header lists, never short-circuits); phase 09's LocalRateLimit is the first production filter to surface the gap.

Per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` D-3.3 ("differential correctness beats internal fidelity") + D-3.5 ("Decisions are written, not remembered"), **ADR-0033** ratified the 4-commit corrective sequence:

- **Commit A (`e9a6cb4`)** — ADR-0033 + SPEC §2.2 revision + PROGRESS mid-execution preamble subsection (docs-only). Voids PLAN lock-ins #13 (429 synth response shape with `x-envoy-ratelimited` + empty body), #30 (`x-envoy-ratelimited` BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT row at Task 5), partial #33 (in-process backstop direct presence assertion). **DECISIONS.md ledger advances `ADR-0032 → ADR-0033`** at this commit.

- **Commit B (`1c1de0f`)** — Task 3 runtime fixup. `LocalRateLimitFilter::decode_headers` drops the `("x-envoy-ratelimited", "true")` header injection from the synth `FilterResponse`; changes `body: Bytes::new()` → `body: Bytes::from_static(b"local_rate_limited")` (18 bytes; static literal; zero-allocation). 2 unit tests updated (`decode_headers_rate_limits_after_max_tokens_and_increments_rate_limited_enforced` drops the x-envoy-ratelimited assertion + adds the body + headers-empty asserts; `decode_headers_appends_configured_response_headers` drops the x-envoy-ratelimited assertion + adds the `headers.len() == 1` + body asserts). Operator-configurable `response_headers_to_add` plumbing PRESERVED.

- **Commit C (`ae2cef0`)** — Task 4 H1 HCM fixup. New `decorate_filter_synth_response(resp: &mut Response, close: bool)` helper at `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs` (immediately before `synth_400`, after `synth_status`); called from both `RequestPath::SynthFromDecode(resp)` arm (decode-side) + `Decision::StopAndSend(replacement)` arm (encode-side filter substitution). The helper conditionally adds 5 standard headers (`server`, `date`, `content-length`, `content-type`, `connection`) if not provided by the filter (case-insensitive name check matching the 06.1 D1 / 08.1 D1 dedupe precedent); `content-length` is ALWAYS set from `body.len()` (overwrites any stale filter-provided value). 2 new unit tests covering the empty-filter-headers happy path + the filter-provides-overlapping-headers edge case (dedupe + overwrite semantic).

- **Commit D (`1384c48`)** — Task 5 re-attempt. Fixture `tests/fixtures/0016-http-filter-local-rate-limit/{envoy.yaml,envoy-rust.yaml,expectations.yaml,README.md}` per the ADR-0033 revised contract (5-probe burst `[200, 200, 200, 429, 429]` + body `"local_rate_limited"` byte_exact on 429 probes + `set_equal_modulo_allow_list` headers). Docker-gated wrapper at `tests/differential/tests/http_filter_local_rate_limit.rs`. Per-side YAML asymmetry per the fixture-0013 precedent: upstream's `envoy.yaml` has an admin block + `filter_enabled`/`filter_enforced: { default_value: { numerator: 100, denominator: HUNDRED } }` (upstream defaults both to 0%; envoy-rust defaults to always-on); envoy-rust.yaml omits both. Both YAMLs use `tokens_per_fill: 3` (upstream rejects 0 per its `TokenBucketValidator`; envoy-rust accepts 0; intersection picked at 3). **NO BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md row** at this commit per PLAN lock-in #30 voiding.

**Tasks 6 + Task 6 follow-up + Task 7: corpus + in-process backstop.**

- **Task 6 (`28e1666`)** — D8.2 parse_bootstrap fuzz corpus seed `hcm_local_rate_limit_filter.yaml` (~45 LoC YAML data) + 1-line `crates/envoy-config/fuzz/.gitignore` allow-list entry per the established curated-seed pattern (the existing 20 named seeds are each listed as `!corpus/parse_bootstrap/<name>.yaml`; this commit extends to 21).
- **Task 6 follow-up (`1effb0f`)** — 1-line extension to `crates/envoy-config/src/bootstrap.rs::tests::fuzz_corpus_seeds_parse_or_reject_cleanly` SUCCESS array to enumerate the new seed (the test enumerates seeds explicitly; without the entry, the unit-test bucket wouldn't exercise the new seed against `parse_bootstrap`).
- **Task 7 (`7fcaeb1`)** — D8.3 in-process backstop `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_local_rate_limit.rs` (~260 LoC). Boots envoy-bin subprocess with synthesized bootstrap (`max_tokens: 2, tokens_per_fill: 2, fill_interval: 60s`); drives 4 sequential `GET /` requests; asserts the ADR-0033 revised contract: status sequence `[200, 200, 429, 429]` + body `"local_rate_limited"` on 429 probes + 5 standard HTTP/1.1 headers (`server`/`date`/`content-length`/`content-type`/`connection`) on 429 probes + NO `x-envoy-ratelimited` on ANY of the 4 probes (per ADR-0033 voiding lock-in #33) + body `"ok\n"` on 200 probes. **Deviations:** `tokens_per_fill: 0 → 2` (PLAN-prescribed `0` changed to `2` for fixture-0016 symmetry; no observable behavior change); `body_lists` captured per ADR-0033 body assertions (PLAN discarded with `_body`); assertion block fully rewritten per ADR-0033 dispatch. Two-stage review code-quality reviewer flagged 2 IMPORTANT items deferred to state-5 housekeeping (subprocess leak risk on probe-helper panic; un-drained stderr pipe — both inherit from the 07.2 in-process backstop precedent's same pattern).

The 09 REVIEW (reviewed range `b9da8d4..a5ebddd`; landed at the state-5 commit `10bb71c`) concluded **Approved with M-track follow-ups** — **zero Critical**, **zero Important**, **5 Minor findings (M1-M5)** + **2 doc-staleness items (D1-D2)** + **3 test/audit-trail polish items (T1-T3)**. SPEC §2.2 had been revised in-place at ADR-0033 Commit A `e9a6cb4`; the §3 D8.3 cross-reference text wasn't synced at that time — now closed. ~1-LoC docstring edit; non-load-bearing doc drift; no test re-run required. The fixture body + expectations.yaml + envoy-rust.yaml docstring already correctly reflected the ADR-0033 contract at Commit D `1384c48`; only the upstream-Envoy-side YAML's docstring header carried the stale claim — now closed. ~1-LoC YAML comment edit; non-load-bearing doc drift; no test re-run required.
