Items carrying forward (the 3 Minor M1-M3 + 2 doc-staleness D1-D2 + 3 test/audit-trail T1-T3 findings — all awareness-only / close-opportunistically; **none has a hard-gated owner phase; none gated the 09 state-6 close-out**):

- **M1 (Minor; `TokenBucketState::try_acquire` CAS-shape race on the refill path).** `crates/envoy-filter/src/local_rate_limit.rs:215-287` — the CAS at line 263 reads `compare_exchange(current, next, ...)` where `current = self.tokens.load(...)` (pre-refill value) and `next = available - 1`. When `available = current + 1` (exactly one interval elapsed AND `tokens_per_fill = 1`), `next` collapses to `current`, making the CAS `compare_exchange(current, current)` — which succeeds unconditionally for any concurrent thread observing the same `current`. Two threads observing the same `current` value in this scenario both succeed their CAS, both return `true`, and the single refill token is "consumed" twice. **Why it matters:** real concurrent double-consumption bug on the refill path. **Why it does NOT rise above Minor at phase-09 scope:** the bug is exercised only when `tokens_per_fill > 0` AND a refill interval elapses concurrently with multiple acquires AND the refill credit per interval is exactly enough to bridge `current → current + 1`. Phase 09's actual exercise paths all avoid the contested arm (fixture 0016 uses `tokens_per_fill: 3` + `fill_interval: 60s` with sub-1s burst; backstop uses `tokens_per_fill: 2` + 60s same shape; torture test uses `tokens_per_fill: 0`). The token cap at `max_tokens` provides a hard ceiling on over-crediting — blast radius bounded. **Fix sketch:** either (a) require `available > current` precondition for the CAS, OR (b) replace `compare_exchange(current, next)` with a token-decrement loop that re-loads on every iteration AND atomically combines the refill-credit with the consume. ~15-30 LoC + an additional torture test exercising the contested path. **Disposition: close at whichever future HTTP-filter-family phase first exercises non-zero `tokens_per_fill` within a refill-window-spanning burst**, OR at a dedicated token-bucket hardening pass. Named owner: future HTTP-filter-family phase (likely global-rate-limit or fault-injection). Non-blocking.

- **M2 (Minor; H2 HCM writer-arm sites don't decorate filter-synth responses with standard HTTP/1.1 response headers; ADR-0033 Consequences misrepresents this as "H2 naturally inherits").** `crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs:373-378` (decode-side `H2RequestPath::SynthFromDecode`) returns the filter's response verbatim; `crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs:436-443` (encode-side `Decision::StopAndSend(replacement)`) constructs `resp` verbatim; both pass through `build_http_response` at `crates/envoy-http2/src/response.rs:29-50` which does NOT add `server` / `date` / `content-type`. An H2 listener exercising the phase-09 LocalRateLimitFilter would emit 429 responses missing those standard headers (the H2-forbidden `connection` correctly stays stripped; `content-length` is implicit from `send_data(end_of_stream=true)`). ADR-0033 Consequences §iii(c)-end (`docs/envoy-rust/DECISIONS.md` ~line 697) claims "the corresponding H2 HCM path naturally inherits via the shared Http1HCMConfig re-export" — but this conflates config re-export with code reuse (H2 writer-arm sites have their own response-construction code that does NOT call `decorate_filter_synth_response`). Will mislead future filter-family phases (fault / ext_authz / oauth2 / rbac) into assuming H2 is already covered. **Why it does NOT rise above Minor at phase-09 scope:** the phase-09 differential surface is HTTP/1.1 only (fixture 0016's `codec_type: HTTP1`); the in-process backstop is HTTP/1.1 only; the H2 gap is genuinely latent and unexercised by phase-09's actual bilateral assertion. **Fix sketch:** either (a) amend ADR-0033 Consequences + PROGRESS Commit C forward-looking note to explicitly record the H2 analogous gap as known-deferred (~10-15 LoC docs edit; preferred per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi) accept-drift), OR (b) land an H2 `decorate_filter_synth_response` analogue (~50-70 LoC + 2 tests; extends the corrective sequence by 1 commit). **Disposition: prefer (a); close at next HTTP-filter-family phase exercising filters on H2** (fault / ext_authz / oauth2 / rbac per ADR-0033 Consequences §5). Named owner: future HTTP-filter-family phase exercising filters on H2. Non-blocking.

- **M3 (Minor; Task 7 in-process backstop subprocess discipline regresses from 07.2 / 08.2 precedents).** `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_local_rate_limit.rs:30` declares `use std::process::{Command, Stdio};` (no `kill_on_drop` API); lines 180-188 configure `.stderr(Stdio::piped()).stdout(Stdio::piped())` (both pipes; no concurrent drain reader). 07.2 + 08.2 backstops both use `tokio::process::Command` + `kill_on_drop(true)` + `Stdio::null()` for stdout. **Why it matters:** (a) any panic in `send_request_and_collect`'s 8 `.expect(...)` / `.unwrap()` sites before explicit `child.kill()` leaks the envoy-bin subprocess; (b) un-drained stdout pipe doubles the OS-pipe-buffer-fill deadlock surface; (c) PROGRESS Task 7's "same pattern as 07.2 backstop" claim is factually wrong — the discipline is a regression from BOTH 07.2 AND 08.2. **Why it does NOT rise above Minor at phase-09 scope:** test-helper-only; no production-code impact; the state-4 CI anchor ran the backstop GREEN with no leaks observed (the test passed cleanly so no panic sites fired); the CI environment's process-lifecycle cleanup mitigates the leak risk on green-test runs. **Fix sketch:** 3-line patch: `use tokio::process::{Command, Stdio};` + `.stdout(Stdio::null())` + `.kill_on_drop(true)`. ~3 LoC; trivially mechanical. **Disposition: close at next HTTP-filter-family phase touching `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_*.rs` backstops**, OR opportunistically. Named owner: future HTTP-filter-family phase touching backstops. Non-blocking; test-helper-only.

- **D1 (Minor; ADR-0033 §iii(c) + PROGRESS Commit A discovery point 3 reference fictional `RequestPath::SynthFromEncode` variant).** The actual `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs::RequestPath` enum has only `Match`, `SynthFromBuild`, and `SynthFromDecode` variants — no `SynthFromEncode`. The implementation is correct (the encode-side decoration site is the `envoy_filter::Decision::StopAndSend(replacement) =>` arm of the encode-side filter dispatch, NOT a separate `SynthFromEncode` variant of `RequestPath`); only the ADR / PROGRESS prose uses fictional variant names; the line-number references are correct. **Disposition: no action; close opportunistically** at whichever future ADR amends or references ADR-0033 (likely never — ADRs are append-only). Named owner: none. Awareness-only narrative cleanup.

- **D2 (Minor; Cargo.lock 2-line metadata delta at Task 2 refines PLAN lock-in #39 from "empty diff" to "metadata-only diff for path-dep additions").** Task 2's `envoy-stats` path-dep on `envoy-filter` produced a 2-line Cargo.lock delta (path-dep metadata only; no new transitive crates pulled). PROGRESS Task 2 deviation #1 honestly discloses this refinement. The 04.1 REVIEW M5/M9 Cargo.lock cadence ratification ADR carries forward unchanged through phase 09 (the cadence pick stays unforced — workspace-internal path-dep additions are not the trigger for the cadence ADR). **Disposition: no action needed.** The refined reading ("metadata-only diff for path-dep additions") is the projection shape for future phases that add workspace-internal path-deps.

- **T1 (Minor; Task 1 `parse_duration` accepts `+`-prefixed numeric values silently).** `crates/envoy-config/src/bootstrap.rs:1825-1842` — `u64::from_str` accepts `"+60"`, `"0060s"`, and similar non-canonical shapes that upstream Envoy v1.33's protobuf Duration parser would reject. No test covers `"+60s"` or `"-1s"`. **Why it does NOT rise above Minor:** the validator at `validate_local_rate_limit_config` runs after parse and rejects bogus durations (`fill_interval > 0` check); a `+60s` input would produce a `Duration` of 60s and behave identically to `60s`. The `-1s` case fails parse cleanly (negative prefix not valid for `u64`). No phase-09 differential surface exercises non-canonical shapes. **Fix sketch:** either (a) explicitly reject `+`/`-` prefixes, OR (b) add tests pinning the accept-or-reject behavior. ~5-10 LoC. **Disposition: close opportunistically** at whichever future phase touches `parse_duration`. Named owner: none.

- **T2 (Minor; Task 2 torture test exercises only `tokens_per_fill = 0` no-refill path; does NOT cover the contested refill-CAS path documented in M1).** The REQUIRED 8-task × 10_000-acquire torture test fixes `tokens_per_fill: 0`, routing through the else branch where the contested CAS arm always represents a real state change. The refill-path CAS-shape race documented in M1 is unexercised by the torture test. **Why it does NOT rise above Minor:** the SPEC §6.3 prescription was honored exactly within its prescribed scope (the torture test asserts the strong invariant `observed == expected` + `final_bucket_count == 0`); the refill-path coverage gap is a SPEC-projection gap, not a code defect. **Fix sketch:** additional torture test with `tokens_per_fill > 0` + short `fill_interval` so multiple intervals elapse during the burst — would catch the M1 CAS-shape race. ~50-80 LoC. **Disposition: close alongside M1** at whichever future HTTP-filter-family phase exercises non-zero `tokens_per_fill`. Named owner: same as M1.

- **T3 (Minor; Task 3 `decode_headers_appends_configured_response_headers` test uses brittle exact-length assertion `headers.len() == 1`).** `crates/envoy-filter/src/local_rate_limit.rs:536` — the test asserts exact length 1 (the one configured `x-rate-limit-policy` entry). If a future commit adds an operator-configurable third header to the filter's own emit path, this length assertion brittlely breaks even if the new behavior is correct. **Why it does NOT rise above Minor:** the test is narrowly scoped to the post-ADR-0033 contract (verifying no spurious `x-envoy-ratelimited` injection); the exact-length check is one way to verify the absence of the spurious header. **Fix sketch:** replace `assert_eq!(resp.headers.len(), 1, ...)` with set-membership check + absence-of-`x-envoy-ratelimited` check. ~3-5 LoC. **Disposition: close opportunistically** at the next phase that touches `crates/envoy-filter/src/local_rate_limit.rs::tests`. Named owner: future HTTP-filter-family phase extending LocalRateLimit's response-header surface.

### Phase-09 ADR ledger (final)

The phase-09 state-3 arc evolved DECISIONS.md ledger head **ADR-0032 → ADR-0033** (1 ADR landed; not the 0 ADRs the phase-09 SPEC §7 + PLAN lock-in #34 projected — ADR-0033 was discovered, not projected, at Task 5 subagent dispatch on `2026-05-17` against HEAD `78128f4` per ADR-0033's Provenance section).

- **ADR-0033** (landed at mid-execution Commit A `e9a6cb4`; ~3300 words at `docs/envoy-rust/DECISIONS.md` lines ~655 onwards) — `Phase-09 SPEC §2.2 revision per upstream Envoy v1.33 empirical observation (drop x-envoy-ratelimited injection; align 429 body + H1 HCM filter-synth header decoration)`. The 4-commit corrective sequence A→B→C→D landed clean per D-3.3 + D-3.5: Commit A docs-only (SPEC §2.2 revision + PROGRESS preamble + PLAN lock-in voidings); Commit B (`1c1de0f`) Task 3 runtime fixup (drop `x-envoy-ratelimited` injection; body `Bytes::from_static(b"local_rate_limited")`); Commit C (`ae2cef0`) Task 4 H1 HCM fixup (new `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper called from BOTH writer-arm sites — `SynthFromDecode` + encode-side `Decision::StopAndSend`); Commit D (`1384c48`) Task 5 re-attempt (fixture 0016 + Docker-gated wrapper per the revised contract). 3 PLAN lock-ins explicitly voided (#13 / #30 / partial #33) per ADR-0033 Decision + Consequences. The H1 HCM `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper is now available to any future HTTP-filter-family phase emitting `Decision::StopAndSend(FilterResponse)` (fault / ext_authz / oauth2 / rbac per ADR-0033 Consequences §5); the H2 HCM analogous decoration site is unimplemented per phase-09 REVIEW M2 carryforward.

- **Conditional ADR-0034** (foundations grant for token-bucket primitive per phase-09 SPEC §7) — stays **reserved-available + UNUSED** through phase 09. The no-foundations-grant posture per phase-09 SPEC §7 + PLAN lock-in #34 held end-to-end (hand-rolled `TokenBucketState` per std-lib `AtomicU64` + `Mutex<Instant>` + `bytes::Bytes` + std-lib `Duration` was sufficient; no `governor` / `ratelimit` / `nonzero-ext` / `parking_lot` / `humantime` / `humantime-serde` / `rand` / `getrandom` foundations grant required). The ADR-0034 reservation was filter-specific to phase-09 and is now released; the next phase that needs a foundations grant or split takes ADR-0034 at its landing site without renumbering — per the standing D-3.5 numbering discipline.

DECISIONS.md ledger head at phase-09 close: **ADR-0033**. Project-cumulative ADR count: 33 ADRs landed across 00-09. The phase-09 ADR ledger evolution `ADR-0032 → ADR-0033` is the first post-MVP-trunk ADR landing; the discovery-not-projection pattern at Task 5 dispatch demonstrates the D-3.5 "Decisions are written, not remembered" + D-3.3 "differential correctness beats internal fidelity" doctrines working as designed under empirical-engagement pressure. Future state-1 brainstorming sessions should empirically verify upstream wire shapes for novel filter surfaces before locking in BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT projections (per ADR-0033 Provenance's recommendation for future SPEC authoring — an awareness-only doctrine-discipline note, not a doctrine-level enforcement).

### Phase-10 state-1 brainstorm

Brainstorm session decisions (THIS commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"). This is the **second post-MVP-trunk feature-family brainstorm session** (after phase-09 state-1 brainstorm `3025594`; the closest shape precedent + cadence-setter). Mirrors phase-09 state-1 brainstorm `3025594` shape exactly.

- **Family pick: HTTP filters family (continuation; second concrete row beneath the family heading).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. The phase-09 brainstorm tiebreaker logic (carryforward-closure value + smallest tractable scope + most mature contract surface) carries forward — applying the same 5-dimension scoring framework at phase-10:

  - **HTTP filters family (WINNER)** — (a) carryforward closure value LOW-MEDIUM (M2 closable via doc-amendment per preferred close shape; M3 mechanical via backstop discipline; both phase-09 carryforwards target HTTP-filter-family by named-owner disposition); (b) zero foundation pressure (header-based RBAC reuses 04.2 HeaderMatcher; no new foundations needed); (c) low-medium arch risk (extends 07.x framework + reuses 09 H1 HCM helper; principal/permission matcher tree is bounded new primitive); (d) HIGH contract maturity (HCM helper + 4-counter pattern + StopAndSend established at phase 09); (e) HIGH-MEDIUM scope tractability (~1100-1300 LoC for header-scoped RBAC under §6.1 gate).
  - **Network filters family** — (a) zero carryforward closure; (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) HIGH arch risk (new network-layer filter chain framework primitive; distinct from HTTP filter chain); (d) LOW contract maturity (no existing network-filter contract entries); (e) VARIES per filter (`echo` ~400-600 LoC + chain framework ~800-1000 LoC). Rejected on arch-risk + contract-maturity dimensions.
  - **Load balancing family** — (a) zero carryforward closure (phase-09 carryforwards don't engage LB seam); (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) LOW arch risk (extends 02.x ClusterLoadBalancer seam; LB algo is pure endpoint-pick); (d) MEDIUM contract maturity (no specific LB stats in BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT yet); (e) HIGH scope tractability (`least_request` ~600-800 LoC). Strong third-place alternative; rejected on the zero-carryforward-closure + less mature contract surface dimensions (the same tiebreaker phase-09 used). The phase-09 brainstorm's "with phase-09 closed, the carryforward-closure dimension is now more balanced" observation pulls LB family closer to viable; the M2 + M3 closures available in HTTP-filter-family still tip the tiebreaker.
  - **Upstream robustness family** — (a) HIGH carryforward closure (closes 06.3 REVIEW I2 — synthetic 5xx backend; the named-owner family); (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) MEDIUM arch risk (health-check task subsystem is new primitive); (d) MEDIUM contract maturity; (e) MEDIUM-tractability scope (~1200-1400 LoC for tightly-scoped active HTTP health checks; at the upper end of §6.1 gate). Rejected on scope-tractability dimension (the upper-end LoC + new task-spawning-per-endpoint primitive combination is higher risk than HTTP-filter-family RBAC's bounded matcher-tree primitive).
  - **HTTP/3 + QUIC family** — VERY HIGH foundation pressure (`quinn` pull) + VERY HIGH arch risk (new UDP listener path; h3spec gate). Rejected per phase-09 brainstorm's identical rejection reasoning.
  - **gRPC family** — `tonic` already permitted but no concrete gRPC backend engaged yet; possible but unmotivated as second standalone phase. Rejected per phase-09 brainstorm's identical rejection.
  - **xDS / dynamic config family** — HIGHEST architectural risk (entire new state machine). Rejected per phase-09 brainstorm's identical rejection.
  - **Observability family** — second-tier alternative at phase-09; same posture applies at phase-10.
  - **Runtime + hot restart family** — hot restart heavy; rejected.
  - **WASM host family** — multi-phase sub-project per §9; rejected.
  - **Deprecated / edge features** — meta-bookkeeping only; not a substantive feature family.

  Top-3 viable: HTTP filters > Load balancing > Upstream robustness. HTTP filters wins on carryforward-closure value (09 REVIEW M2 + M3 close at named sites) + smallest tractable scope-without-foundations (~1100-1300 LoC; no foundations grant) + most mature contract surface (09's HCM helper + 4-counter pattern directly applies). The phase-10 SPEC §1 + §3 record the picked family + first-filter scope.

- **First filter pick: `envoy.filters.http.rbac` (header-based Permission/Principal scope).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Per the §9 HTTP-filter sub-feature enumeration (header_mutation DONE at 07.2; local_ratelimit DONE at 09), considered the remaining ~14 candidate filters along (config shape / foundations needed / framework extension needed / carryforward closure):

  - **rbac (WINNER — header-based scope)** — chain-config; no per-route required at phase-10 scope; ZERO foundations (reuses 04.2 HeaderMatcher); ZERO framework extension (Decision::StopAndSend exists from 07.1; H1 HCM `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper exists from 09 ADR-0033 Commit C); closes 09 REVIEW M2 + M3 at named-owner sites; ~1100-1300 LoC including tests + fixture (under §6.1 gate). Header-only Permission/Principal types (Any + Header + AndRules/AndIds + OrRules/OrIds + NotRule/NotId combinators); IP/CIDR + metadata + authenticated + url_path + SNI + CEL + shadow_rules all defer per SPEC §4.
  - **fault (abort-only)** — runner-up; ~700-900 LoC; needs `rand` foundations grant (ADR-0034) OR hand-rolled XorShift32 PRNG (~30-50 LoC primitive); closes 09 M2 + M3 if H1 backstop. Rejected on foundation-pressure dimension — `rand` grant or hand-rolled PRNG primitive both add risk beyond rbac's reuse-only posture.
  - **cors** — needs per-route framework extension (CorsPolicy is primary per-route in upstream Envoy); ~1000-1200 LoC + per-route primitive (~200-300 LoC). Rejected on arch-risk dimension — per-route primitive is load-bearing for many later filters but higher risk than rbac's bounded matcher tree.
  - **rbac (IP-included scope)** — would close 09 M2 + M3 + more; needs IP/CIDR matcher primitive (not currently in envoy-config). Rejected — IP matcher primitive defers per the standing principle that new framework primitives are gating architectural changes; header-scoped rbac is the minimum-viable cut.
  - **buffer** — tiny config; needs new body-buffering framework extension (larger than the filter itself). Rejected.
  - **csrf** — per-route primary; same per-route extension as cors. Rejected.
  - **compression** — needs `flate2`/`brotli`/`zstd` foundations grants + new pluggable-compressor primitive. Rejected.
  - **jwt_authn** — needs `jsonwebtoken` foundations grant + JOSE primitive. Rejected.
  - **ext_authz / ext_proc** — needs gRPC client (depends on gRPC family). Rejected.
  - **oauth2** — needs HTTPS client + auth-state framework. Rejected.
  - **adaptive concurrency** — sophisticated gradient algorithm. Rejected.
  - **admission control** — probabilistic + stats-heavy (P50/P99). Rejected.
  - **bandwidth limit** — per-stream timing; needs body-streaming framework extension. Rejected on framework-extension dimension despite the appealing M1+T2 closure opportunity (TokenBucketState reuse with bytes-as-tokens).
  - **global rate limit** — depends on gRPC family. Rejected.
  - **lua / wasm** — own multi-phase sub-projects per §9. Rejected.

  Top-3 viable: rbac (header-scoped) > fault (abort-only) > cors. **rbac (header-scoped) wins** on: zero foundations grant (reuses 04.2 HeaderMatcher directly); zero framework extension (uses existing H1 HCM helper + StopAndSend); closes 09 REVIEW M2 + M3 at named sites; validates StopAndSend on a non-rate-limit semantic (deny-by-policy vs rate-exhaustion); ~1100-1300 LoC under §6.1 gate. The 10 SPEC §3 D3 records the recursive tree-walk evaluator design; SPEC §6.2 makes the §6.2 empirical verification at state-2 PLAN-write REQUIRED per ADR-0033 process-gap doctrine (verify stats namespace + 403 body bytes + 403 header set against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` Docker before locking PLAN lock-ins).

- **Phase id + slug pick:** **`10`** / **`10-http-filter-rbac`**. The id `10` is next sequential after `09` per the project's numbering convention (the next standalone-phase id is the next integer; sub-phases get `.N` suffixes only on split). The slug mirrors `09-http-filter-local-rate-limit`'s hyphenation pattern — readable English (`http-filter-rbac`) over upstream-name verbatim. - **46 architecture-decision lock-ins** per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Full table in PLAN.md §2; grouped summary in PROGRESS.md Task 1 preamble. Key non-obvious choices:

  - **Recursive tree-walk evaluator shape (#3-#10)** — `RbacFilter { action: RuntimeAction, policies: Arc<Vec<RuntimePolicy>>, allowed_counter: Arc<Counter>, denied_counter: Arc<Counter> }`; `RuntimePermission` + `RuntimePrincipal` mirror enums with `Any(bool) / Header(HeaderMatcher) / AndRules-AndIds(Vec) / OrRules-OrIds(Vec) / NotRule-NotId(Box)`; `eval_permission` + `eval_principal` recursive descent with `Iterator::all` (AND short-circuit) + `Iterator::any` (OR short-circuit) + recursive `!eval_*` for Not; pure-compute synchronous (no I/O; no async). Stack-bounded by parse-time `RBAC_TREE_MAX_DEPTH = 16`.
  - **Decision computation (#10)** — short-circuit on first matching policy across `BTreeMap` alphabetical iteration; per-policy `permissions.iter().any() && principals.iter().any()` (permissions-OR-list + principals-OR-list at the top of each Policy; AND-list semantics live inside `AndRules`/`AndIds` wrappers per upstream proto). Action-vs-match decision matrix per SPEC §5.6: `Allow+match → ALLOW`, `Allow+no_match → DENY`, `Deny+match → DENY`, `Deny+no_match → ALLOW`. Counter increments EXACTLY ONCE per request at the decision site.
  - **403 synth response + body bytes (#13-#14)** — `FilterResponse { status: 403, reason: Some("Forbidden"), headers: vec![], body: Bytes::from_static(b"RBAC: access denied") }` — **19 bytes, NO trailing newline per ADR-0034 §6.2 empirical-verification finding**. The 5 standard HTTP/1.1 headers decorated by `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper at H1 HCM (landed at phase-09 ADR-0033 Commit C `ae2cef0`). Phase-10 is the **first non-LocalRateLimit consumer** of the helper.
  - **Counter registration namespace (#15)** — `format!("http.{hcm_stat_prefix}.rbac.{allowed,denied}")` per upstream Envoy v1.33 parity (verified at §6.2). The `hcm_stat_prefix` is threaded from `Http1HCMConfig::from_config` at `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:185` through `FilterPipeline::build_from_config` → `HttpFilterInstance::build` → `RbacFilter::build_from_config` (SIGNATURE WIDENING: phase-09 widened to `(filters, &registry)`; phase-10 widens further to `(filters, &registry, hcm_stat_prefix: &str)` — single H1 HCM call site to widen).
  - **envoy-config schema (#16-#25)** — `RbacConfig { rules: Rules }` + `Rules { action: Action (default Allow), policies: BTreeMap<String, Policy> }` + `Policy { permissions: Vec<Permission>, principals: Vec<Principal> }` + symmetric Permission/Principal enums with `#[serde(rename = "any|header|and_rules|or_rules|not_rule|and_ids|or_ids|not_id")]` field-name oneof; `Box<Permission>` for NotRule (and `Box<Principal>` for NotId) to break the recursive enum size. `BTreeMap` for deterministic cross-proxy iteration per SPEC §5.6. Reuses 04.2 HeaderMatcher directly via `envoy_config::HeaderMatcher` import.
  - **6 new ConfigError variants (#26)** — `EmptyRbacPolicies`, `EmptyRbacPolicyPermissions`, `EmptyRbacPolicyPrincipals`, `EmptyRbacPermissionSet`, `EmptyRbacPrincipalSet`, `RbacTreeTooDeep`. All carry `listener: String`; the policy/path-bearing variants also carry `policy_name: String` + `path: String`. Land in `crates/envoy-config/src/lib.rs` alongside existing HeaderMutation + LocalRateLimit variants per PLAN §1 SPEC correction #2.
  - **`RBAC_TREE_MAX_DEPTH = 16` constant (#27)** — defense-in-depth bound on recursion at parse time per SPEC §3 D2.
  - **D5 + D6 + D7 task organization (#31-#32 + #36-#37)** — D6 + D7.1 (2 stat rows) co-locate at Task 3 per SPEC §6.6; D5 + D7.2 (ADR-0033 Consequences amendment + phase-09 PROGRESS Commit C cross-ref) co-locate at Task 4 per SPEC §6.3. D5 lands as in-place amendment to ADR-0033 Consequences §iii(c)-end (NOT as a superseding ADR-0035 per SPEC §7 option D recommended posture; the change is a clarification, not a Decision shift).

Task 7 implementer reads 07.2 + 08.2 backstop precedents via direct `Read` tool spot-check before writing per 09 REVIEW M3 Process note + PLAN lock-in #40.

Remaining 3 conditional ADR-0034 slots (option B per-route deferral; option C foundations grant; option D D5 superseding-ADR shape) all DEFER per recommended posture. Next available number: ADR-0035.

- **§6.2 empirical-verification findings (3 — performed at THIS state-2 PLAN-write commit per phase-10 SPEC §6.2 + ADR-0033 process-gap-awareness doctrine):**

  - **Finding (a) — Stats namespace shape: MATCHES** SPEC §2.1 projection. Empirical Docker run of `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` against the SPEC §3 D8.1 canonical bootstrap (1 ALLOW policy `pass_with_header`; 1 allowed + 1 denied probe) yielded `/stats` scrape: `http.ingress_http.rbac.{allowed,denied}` at 1 each, plus `shadow_allowed`/`shadow_denied` at 0 unconditionally (shadow_rules counters are emitted even when shadow_rules is unconfigured per upstream v1.33). Phase-10 registers only the 2 primary counters (shadow_rules defers per SPEC §4); the differential fixture does NOT scrape RBAC stats (only the 4 HTTP status probes) so the 2-vs-4 name-set divergence is not exercised bilaterally. **No SPEC revision; no PLAN lock-in voided.**

  - **Finding (b) — 403 response body bytes: DIFFERS by 1 byte** from SPEC §2.2 + §3 D8.1 + §5.9 projection. Empirical body bytes (under both `curl -i` default-keep-alive AND Python TCP client mirroring `drive_http_get`'s `Connection: close` framing — identical body): `content-length: 19` + body hex `52 42 41 43 3a 20 61 63 63 65 73 73 20 64 65 6e 69 65 64` = `"RBAC: access denied"` (19 bytes, NO trailing newline). SPEC projected `"RBAC: access denied\n"` (20 bytes). **MATERIAL DIFFERENCE.** **Disposition: ADR-0034 (option A per SPEC §7) lands inline at THIS state-2 PLAN-write commit.** The ADR records the empirical evidence + the 6 inline SPEC.md revisions (each replaces 20-byte projection with 19-byte reality + cross-refs ADR-0034). PLAN lock-ins #13 + #14 lock the production-code shape `body: Bytes::from_static(b"RBAC: access denied")`.

  - **Finding (c) — 403 response header set under harness `Connection: close` framing: MATCHES** SPEC §2.2 projection. 5 headers `{content-length: 19, content-type: text/plain, date: ..., server: envoy, connection: close}`. The `connection: close` appears because the harness sends `Connection: close` per `drive_http_get` at line 1039; if a client sends keep-alive (or default HTTP/1.1 keep-alive), upstream omits `connection` (verified with `curl -i` default — observed 4 headers, no `connection`). envoy-rust's `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper adds the same 5 headers under the harness framing. **No SPEC revision needed.**

  This is the **first phase to leverage the SPEC §6.2 empirical-verification process-improvement** directly — phase-09 surfaced the analogous ADR-0033 only at Task 5 subagent dispatch (a process gap the ADR-0033 Provenance section called out). Phase-10's state-2 PLAN-write performs the verification BEFORE locking PLAN lock-ins, AVOIDING the process gap. Validates that the verify-at-PLAN-write discipline works as intended.

- **7 PLAN-write SPEC corrections** (each verified against HEAD `c73f44f` — the phase-10 state-1 brainstorm commit; full text in PROGRESS Task 1 preamble):

  1. `HeaderMatcher::matches` takes `&[(String, String)]`, NOT `&[Header]` as SPEC §3 D3 prose implies. The 04.2-landed signature at `crates/envoy-config/src/matcher.rs:19` is `pub fn matches(&self, headers: &[(String, String)]) -> bool`. `FilterRequest::headers: Vec<(String, String)>` matches directly — no adapter needed.

Validator function `validate_http_filters` IS in `bootstrap.rs` (line 1661). 6 new RBAC ConfigError variants land in `lib.rs`; sub-validator + Rbac dispatch arm land in `bootstrap.rs`.
  3. HCM filter-pipeline build site is `Http1HCMConfig::from_config` at `crates/envoy-http1/src/hcm.rs:185` (same correction as phase-09 PLAN §1 item 2). Phase 10 widens phase-09's `(filters, &registry)` → `(filters, &registry, &cfg.stat_prefix)` — single H1 call site to widen.

New precedent for any filter whose stat namespace embeds the HCM's stat_prefix at register time.
  6. **Empirical-verification body-bytes correction per ADR-0034** — SPEC §2.2 + §3 D8.1 + §5.9 project `"RBAC: access denied\n"` (20 bytes); §6.2 empirical verification reveals `"RBAC: access denied"` (19 bytes; NO trailing newline). ADR-0034 lands at THIS state-2 PLAN-write commit; PLAN lock-in #14 locks production-code shape; 6 SPEC inline edits ratify the revised body.

Added `HttpFilterTypedConfig::Rbac` variant + 8 new schema items (`RbacConfig`, `Rules`, `Action { Allow, Deny }`, `Policy`, `Permission`, `PermissionSet`, `Principal`, `PrincipalSet`) + 6 new `ConfigError` variants (`EmptyRbacPolicies`, `EmptyRbacPolicyPermissions`, `EmptyRbacPolicyPrincipals`, `EmptyRbacPermissionSet`, `EmptyRbacPrincipalSet`, `RbacTreeTooDeep`) + `RBAC_TREE_MAX_DEPTH = 16` constant + 3 sub-validators (`validate_rbac_config`, `validate_permission_tree`, `validate_principal_tree`) + 26 unit tests in `rbac_tests` submodule + transient `instance.rs` bridge arm returning `FilterError::UnsupportedFilterType` for Rbac during the Tasks 1-3 interim window. Reused 04.2 `HeaderMatcher` directly via `envoy_config::HeaderMatcher` import. 851 / -4 LoC.

- **Task 2 (`14a842c`)** — D3 hand-rolled recursive tree-walk evaluator at `crates/envoy-filter/src/rbac.rs` (new module; 243 LoC). `RuntimePermission` + `RuntimePrincipal` mirror enums with `Any(bool) / Header(HeaderMatcher) / AndRules-AndIds(Vec) / OrRules-OrIds(Vec) / NotRule-NotId(Box)`; `eval_permission` + `eval_principal` recursive descent with `Iterator::all` (AND short-circuit) + `Iterator::any` (OR short-circuit) + recursive `!eval_*` for Not. Pure-compute synchronous (no I/O; no async). Stack-bounded by parse-time `RBAC_TREE_MAX_DEPTH = 16`. 12 unit tests. 660 / -1 LoC.

- **Task 3 (`da32137` + SHA-record fixup `65181c1`)** — D3 `RbacFilter` runtime + D6 2-counter stats wiring + D7.1 2 BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md Stat-name mapping rows. `RbacFilter { action, policies: Arc<Vec<RuntimePolicy>>, allowed_counter: Arc<Counter>, denied_counter: Arc<Counter> }` + `build_from_config(cfg, registry, hcm_stat_prefix)` (3-arg signature; new precedent for any filter whose stat namespace embeds the HCM's stat_prefix at register time) + `decode_headers` + `encode_headers`. Stat namespace `http.<hcm_stat_prefix>.rbac.{allowed,denied}` matches upstream Envoy v1.33 parity (verified at §6.2). Counter increments EXACTLY ONCE per request at the decision site. 6 new unit tests; 2 new BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md `Stat-name mapping` rows landed at THIS task commit per PLAN lock-in #36 + SPEC §6.6 cadence. 578 / -21 LoC.

### Phase-10 ADR ledger (final)

The phase-10 state-1 → state-5 arc evolved DECISIONS.md ledger head **ADR-0033 → ADR-0034** (1 ADR landed at state-2 PLAN-write `55abc61`; the projected SPEC §7 option A ADR — empirical-verification 1-byte 403-body correction).

The 6 inline SPEC.md edits + the PLAN.md lock-in #14 + the PROGRESS Task 1 preamble all reference the 19-byte body bytes from PLAN-write onward. **This is the first ADR landed inline at a state-2 PLAN-write commit per the SPEC §6.2 empirical-verification process-improvement** — validates that the verify-at-PLAN-write discipline works as intended (the projected 1-byte correction was caught BEFORE state-3 execution, avoiding the phase-09-style mid-execution corrective sequence). The remaining 3 conditional ADR-0034 slots per SPEC §7 (option B per-route deferral; option C foundations grant; option D D5 superseding-ADR shape) all DEFERRED per recommended posture — option D's D5 amendment landed as in-place ADR-0033 amendment at Task 4 `84b508e` rather than a superseding ADR-0035 (clarification, not a Decision shift; ledger head unchanged).

- **No further ADRs landed during the phase-10 state-3 + state-5 arcs** per PLAN lock-in #41 + the established 06.x / 07.x / 08.x / 09 cadence (state-3 task commits + state-5 REVIEW.md commits NEVER land ADRs unless triggered by mid-execution empirical discovery — phase-10 had no such discovery thanks to the SPEC §6.2 process-improvement).

DECISIONS.md ledger head at phase-10 close (THIS state-5 commit; pending state-6 close-out): **ADR-0034**. Project-cumulative ADR count: 34 ADRs landed across 00-10. The phase-10 ADR ledger evolution `ADR-0033 → ADR-0034` is the second post-MVP-trunk ADR landing; the projected-not-discovered pattern at state-2 PLAN-write demonstrates the SPEC §6.2 empirical-verification process-improvement working as designed. Future state-1 brainstorming sessions for novel filter surfaces should follow the phase-10 pattern (verify upstream wire shapes empirically at state-2 PLAN-write; land any necessary inline ADR at the same commit; avoid mid-execution discovery).

### Phase-11 state-1 brainstorm

Brainstorm session decisions (THIS commit), all per the user's standing preferences `feedback_pick_recommendation` ("always pick the recommended option; do not ask") and `feedback_execution_style` ("default to subagent-driven-development; skip the two-option fork"). This is the **third post-MVP-trunk feature-family brainstorm session** (after phase-09 state-1 brainstorm `3025594` + phase-10 state-1 brainstorm `c73f44f`; the latter is the freshest shape precedent). Mirrors the phase-10 brainstorm shape. The pick was made directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation` — no two-option fork; the alternatives + 5-dimension scoring are documented below per the established brainstorm-subsection cadence.

- **Family pick: HTTP filters family (continuation; third concrete row beneath the family heading).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Applying the same 5-dimension scoring framework used at phases 09 + 10:

  - **HTTP filters family (WINNER)** — (a) carryforward closure value **HIGH** — phase 11 is the **named close site for 09 REVIEW M2** (the H2 HCM filter-synth header-decoration gap, implementation arm — the single most-named open carryforward; the phase-10 D5 ADR-0033 Consequences amendment at `DECISIONS.md:699` named "next HTTP-filter-family phase exercising filters bilaterally on H2" as the close site verbatim). This is the decisive dimension. (b) foundation pressure **LOW** — abort reuses `Decision::StopAndSend` (07.1) + the H1 `decorate_filter_synth_response` helper (09) as the structural template for the new H2 analogue + the 04.2 `HeaderMatcher` for the gate; the only new shared type is `FractionalPercent` (envoy-config, hand-authored). (c) arch risk **LOW-MEDIUM** — abort is a twice-validated pattern (09 + 10); the new surface is the H2 `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` helper (~50-70 LoC + 2 tests, bounded + precedented by the H1 helper). (d) contract maturity **HIGH** — StopAndSend + decoration + per-filter counter wiring established at 09/10. (e) scope tractability **HIGH-MEDIUM** — ~1350-1500 LoC at-or-under the §6.1 gate.
  - **Load balancing family** — (a) carryforward closure **ZERO** (the LB seam doesn't engage the H2-decoration gap or any open carryforward); (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) LOW arch risk (extends the 02.x ClusterLoadBalancer seam; pure endpoint-pick); (d) MEDIUM contract maturity (no specific LB stats in BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT yet); (e) HIGH scope tractability (`least_request` ~600-800 LoC). **Strong second-place alternative** — rejected on the decisive carryforward-closure dimension (it leaves the most-named open carryforward, 09 M2, unclosed) + the lower contract maturity. The phase-10 brainstorm reached the identical disposition.
  - **Upstream robustness family** — (a) carryforward closure MEDIUM (closes 06.3 REVIEW I2 synthetic-5xx-backend, the named-owner family); (b) zero foundation pressure; (c) MEDIUM arch risk (active-health-check task subsystem is a new spawning primitive); (d) MEDIUM contract maturity; (e) MEDIUM scope (~1200-1400 LoC at the upper band). Rejected on the arch-risk + scope dimensions (the new task-spawning primitive is higher risk than fault's reuse-only abort path) + it does not close the higher-value 09 M2 H2 gap.
  - **Network filters family** — HIGH arch risk (new network-layer filter-chain primitive distinct from the HTTP filter chain) + LOW contract maturity (no network-filter contract entries). Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **HTTP/3 + QUIC family** — VERY HIGH foundation pressure (`quinn` pull) + VERY HIGH arch risk (new UDP listener path; h3spec gate). Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **gRPC family** — `tonic` permitted but no concrete gRPC backend engaged; unmotivated as the next standalone phase. Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **xDS / dynamic config family** — HIGHEST architectural risk (entire new state machine). Rejected per the phase-09 + phase-10 identical reasoning.
  - **Observability family** — second-tier alternative; same posture as phases 09 + 10.
  - **Runtime + hot restart family** — hot restart heavy; rejected. (Note: phase-11 fault's runtime-keyed percentage overrides defer specifically because the runtime layer is unimplemented — see SPEC §4.)
  - **WASM host family** — multi-phase sub-project per §9; rejected.
  - **Deprecated / edge features** — meta-bookkeeping only; not a substantive feature family.

  Top-3 viable: **HTTP filters > Load balancing > Upstream robustness.** HTTP filters wins decisively on carryforward-closure value (it is the named close site for the most-named open carryforward, 09 REVIEW M2's implementation arm) + smallest tractable scope-without-foundations + most mature contract surface. The phase-11 SPEC §1 + §3 record the picked family + filter scope.

- **Filter pick: `envoy.filters.http.fault` (abort path; header-gated; deterministic percentage).** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. The decisive sub-consideration: **closing 09 REVIEW M2 (the H2 HCM decoration implementation arm) requires a phase that exercises a `StopAndSend`-capable HTTP filter on an H2 listener.** Among the StopAndSend-capable candidate filters (the natural vehicles for the H2-decoration close), fault (abort) is the lowest-foundation + most-tractable NEW one. Per the §9 HTTP-filter sub-feature enumeration (header_mutation DONE at 07.2; local_ratelimit DONE at 09; rbac DONE at 10), considered the remaining ~14 candidates along (StopAndSend-capable? / foundations needed / framework extension needed / differential-testable):

  - **fault — abort path (WINNER)** — StopAndSend-capable (abort short-circuits via 503); ZERO foundations (reuses StopAndSend + the H1 helper template + 04.2 HeaderMatcher; `FractionalPercent` is a small hand-authored envoy-config type); the ONLY genuinely new surface is the bounded H2 `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` helper (the 09 M2 close); the deterministic 0%/100% abort path is fully differential-testable; ~1350-1500 LoC under the §6.1 gate. The documented phase-10 runner-up. Scoped to abort-only; **fractional percentage + delay + response_rate_limit + grpc_status + downstream-controlled faults + max_active_faults defer per SPEC §4** — notably fractional percentage defers because a per-request probabilistic abort decision is **non-differential-testable** (the differential contract §7.2 does not compare probabilistic per-request outcomes across two independent proxies), and delay defers because timing is not compared by default per §7.2.
  - **jwt_authn (reject → 401)** — StopAndSend-capable + would close 09 M2 if exercised on H2; but needs a JOSE/`jsonwebtoken` foundations grant + JWKS-fetch framework. Rejected on foundation-pressure.
  - **cors (preflight → 200/204)** — StopAndSend-capable for OPTIONS preflight; but CORS is primary per-route in upstream Envoy → needs the per-route framework extension (a ~200-300 LoC gating primitive). Rejected on arch-risk; CORS remains the natural per-route-config close site per the family-wide deferral.
  - **csrf (reject → 403)** — per-route primary; same per-route extension as cors. Rejected.
  - **oauth2 (redirect)** — needs an HTTPS client + auth-state framework. Rejected.
  - **ext_authz / ext_proc** — need a gRPC client (depends on the gRPC family). Rejected.
  - **buffer / bandwidth_limit / compression / response-rate-limit** — need a body-buffering/streaming framework extension larger than the filter. Rejected.
  - **global rate limit** — depends on the gRPC family. Rejected.
  - **adaptive concurrency / admission control** — sophisticated + probabilistic/stats-heavy; non-differential-testable core. Rejected.
  - **lua / wasm** — own multi-phase sub-projects per §9. Rejected.

  Top-3 viable: **fault (abort) > jwt_authn > cors.** **fault (abort) wins** on: zero foundations grant; the lowest-risk vehicle for the 09 M2 H2-decoration close; deterministic abort fully differential-testable; ~1350-1500 LoC under the §6.1 gate. The SPEC §3 D6 records the H2 decoration helper (the centerpiece); SPEC §6.2 makes the empirical verification at state-2 PLAN-write REQUIRED for the fault stats namespace + abort body bytes + H2 abort header set against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` (the phase-10-ratified verify-at-PLAN-write process improvement).

- **Scope-cut decision (fractional percentage deferral):** Picked directly per `feedback_pick_recommendation`. Phase 11 supports **deterministic 0%/100% percentage only**; the validator rejects fractional (`0 < numerator < denominator`) per SPEC §4 + §5.6. **Rationale:** a fractional per-request abort decision depends on a random draw that cannot be matched across two independent proxies, so it is non-differential-testable per the differential contract §7.2; landing it would be untested (un-differential-verifiable) code per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §6.3. Deferring it (a) keeps phase 11 entirely inside the differential contract, (b) avoids landing a hand-rolled per-request PRNG that no fixture can exercise bilaterally, and (c) keeps the phase under the §6.1 gate. The deterministic 0%/100% path needs no PRNG. Fractional defers to a future fault-enrichment phase that hand-rolls the PRNG per D-3.2 + opts into statistical-distribution unit testing per §7.2 (consistent with the rbac "header-based scope only; IP/CIDR defers" minimum-viable-cut discipline at phase 10).

- **Phase id + slug pick:** **`11`** / **`11-http-filter-fault`**. The id `11` is next sequential after `10` per the project's numbering convention (the next standalone-phase id is the next integer; sub-phases get `.N` suffixes only on split). The slug mirrors the `10-http-filter-rbac` + `09-http-filter-local-rate-limit` hyphenation pattern — readable English (`http-filter-fault`) over the upstream-name verbatim. The filter's typed_config `name:` field uses upstream's exact `envoy.filters.http.fault` (typed_config `@type` `…fault.v3.HTTPFault`); the slug + readable name + upstream config name are deliberately distinct surfaces per the 07.2 / 09 / 10 precedent.

- **depends-on pick:** **`07`** (the parent filter-chain framework). Per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 depends-on doctrine (HTTP-filter-family extensions name `07`). **An implicit dependency on phase `05` (the HTTP/2 codec + `Driver::Http2` harness primitive) is load-bearing for the first time in the HTTP-filter family** (phase 11's fixture runs on an H2 listener) — but per ROADMAP schema conventions only direct ROADMAP-row deps are captured in the field; cross-deliverable reuse (05 H2 codec, 04.2 HeaderMatcher, 09 H1 helper template) is implicit. The 17-Docker-gated-fixture regression baseline established at phase-10 close (`0001-tcp-echo` through `0017-http-filter-rbac`) carries forward per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §7.5 (b).

- **Split-gate projection at state-2 (SPEC §6.1):** Single-phase, no split. Per SPEC §3 deliverable enumeration: ~10-12 tasks / ~1350-1500 LoC (production ~620, tests ~700, fixture/harness/doc ~280). At-or-under the §6.1 ~25-task / ~1500-LoC gate; task count comfortably under. **Recommended posture: accept the projection; do NOT nest-split.** Release valve if state-3 drifts over ~1600 LoC: use SPEC §3 D8.1 option (b) (single-probe `Driver::Http2` abort assertion + pass-through covered by the in-process backstop, saving the ~90 LoC `Driver::Http2ProbeList` extension) before any nest-split, per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi).

- **ADR projection at state-1: NONE.** Recommended posture per SPEC §7 — no foundations grants required at any phase-11 lifecycle state (abort reuses existing primitives; `FractionalPercent` is hand-authored envoy-config; the H2 helper is hand-rolled per D-3.2). DECISIONS.md ledger head stays at **ADR-0034** through this state-1 commit; next available **ADR-0035**. Four conditional ADR-0035 slots stay reserved-available for state-2 / state-3 landing (per SPEC §7): option A (PLAN-write §6.2 empirical-verification revision — **the most likely trigger**, given the phase-10 RBAC body was off by 1 byte; the fault abort-body bytes are the likeliest correction); option B (fractional-deferral durability ADR — recommended DEFER; the §4 + §5.6 rationale is self-contained); option C (foundations grant — no grant projected); option D (`Driver::Http2ProbeList` harness-primitive ADR — recommended no-ADR, per the `Driver::Http1ProbeList`/`Driver::AdminScrape` no-ADR harness-extension cadence). At most ONE of A/B/C/D lands at any single commit per D-3.5 sequential numbering.

- **Carryforward closure projection at state-1: 09 REVIEW M2 (implementation arm) closes at phase-11 D6.** Phase 10's D5 closed M2's documentation arm (the ADR-0033 Consequences amendment at `DECISIONS.md:699` naming this phase as the implementation close site). Phase 11's D6 lands the implementation arm: a new `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` helper (~50-70 LoC + 2 tests, the amendment's named size) symmetric to the H1 helper, wired into both H2 HCM synth writer-arm sites (`crates/envoy-http2/src/hcm.rs:373` decode-side `SynthFromDecode` + `:436` encode-side `StopAndSend`), exercised bilaterally by fixture 0018 on an H2 listener. **After D6 lands, the 09 → 10 → 11 M2 chain ENDS.** No new ADR is required to close M2 (the close shape is ordinary implementation deliverable work, not a decision).

- **No other carryforwards engaged in 11 (besides the phase-10 M1 lesson heeded proactively).** Phase-10 M1 (the RBAC backstop omitted the per-probe 5-header presence assertion as an undisclosed deviation) is heeded proactively at phase-11 D8.3 per SPEC §6.4 (the fault backstop SHOULD include the per-probe standard-header presence assertion on the 503 probes, OR disclose its omission). Standing inventory carries forward unchanged: 10 M2 (`hcm.rs:~187` H1 call-site doc-comment — phase 11 widens NO H1 signature, so likely not touched; close opportunistically); 10 M3/M4/D1/D2/T1/T2/T3; 09 REVIEW M1 + T2 (token-bucket CAS race / refill-path coverage — not engaged by fault; no token bucket); 09 REVIEW D1/D2/T1/T3; 08.2 REVIEW M1-M8 + T1-T3; 08.1 REVIEW M3; 07.2 REVIEW M2 + M3; 06.3 REVIEW I2 (upstream-robustness named owner; not engaged); 06.2 REVIEW M1/M2/M4/M5; 06.1 REVIEW M2/M3/M5/M6; 05.3 REVIEW I2; 05.2 REVIEW I1/I2/I3; 04.1 REVIEW M5/M9/M-claim/M1/M2/M4/M7; 02.2 REVIEW M1; Phase-00 I3 SIGKILL→SIGTERM — all carry forward indefinitely per their existing named-owner dispositions.

- **Fixture 0018 projection:** `tests/fixtures/0018-http-filter-fault/` lands at SPEC §3 D8.1 — the **first HTTP-filter-family fixture on an HTTP/2 listener** (the deliberate inverse of the 09 + 10 H1-only fixtures). Bootstrap mirrors fixture 0009's H2 HCM + `direct_response` shape (`codec_type: HTTP2`); fault config `abort: { http_status: 503, percentage: { numerator: 100, denominator: HUNDRED } }` gated by `headers: [- name: x-fault, string_match: { exact: abort }]`. A 4-probe H2 burst alternates the gate header → expected status sequence `[503, 200, 503, 200]`. 503 responses carry the standard H2 headers `{server, date, content-length, content-type}` (NO `connection` — H2-forbidden) via `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` + the abort body byte-exact (exact bytes §6.2-verified at state-2). **Harness:** recommended new `Driver::Http2ProbeList` (mirrors `Driver::Http1ProbeList`; the first multi-probe H2 differential driver) — PLAN-writer confirms the `Driver::Http2` internals + decides between the probe-list driver (recommended; both arms bilateral) or a single-probe abort assertion (the §6.1 trim). Docker-gated wrapper at `tests/differential/tests/http_filter_fault.rs` mirrors the 10 `http_filter_rbac.rs` shape. Fuzz corpus seed `crates/envoy-config/fuzz/corpus/parse_bootstrap/hcm_fault_filter.yaml` extends the corpus 17 → 18 + extends the SUCCESS array per the 09 + 10 Task-6 follow-up precedent. In-process backstop `crates/envoy-bin/tests/http_filter_fault.rs` exercises the H1 abort path (cheap H1-codec coverage to complement the H2 fixture) with the 09 REVIEW M3 `tokio::process::Command + kill_on_drop + Stdio::null` discipline already baked in (M3 closed at phase 10; now the standing pattern).

- **BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md extension projection:** 1 new "Stat-name mapping" row (`http.<hcm_stat_prefix>.fault.aborts_injected` — value-exact; §6.2-verified at state-2) lands at the D7 task commit. No new Header allow-list row needed per SPEC §2.2 (the 04.1-landed `server` + `date` rows cover the cross-proxy implementation-identifying differences; `content-length` + `content-type` are value-exact under the deterministic fixture-0018 burst). Per the 06.x / 07.x / 08.x / 09 / 10 doctrine: contract extensions land at the task where each is first empirically exercised, NOT at PLAN-write time and NOT at state-1 SPEC time.

### Phase-11 state-2 PLAN-write

This subsection records the phase-11 state-2 standalone PLAN-write commit (`phase 11: state-2 standalone PLAN.md`) — the third post-MVP-trunk feature-family state-2 PLAN-write (after phase-09 `b9da8d4` + phase-10 `55abc61`). It lands `PLAN.md` (8 tasks; 42 architecture lock-ins; full `- [ ]` checkbox steps with complete code per task) + `PROGRESS.md` skeleton + Task 1 preamble, flips ROADMAP row `11` `planned` → `in-progress`, and advances STATE.md to state-3-next. Docs-only; 4 files; no ADR; no SPEC edit.

**§6.2 empirical-verification findings (all 3 MATCH — no inline ADR-0035).** Performed at THIS commit against `envoyproxy/envoy:v1.33.0` Docker on an H2 listener (the SPEC §3 D8.1 canonical bootstrap: HCM `codec_type: HTTP2` + fault abort 503 @ 100% gated by `x-fault: abort` + router + direct_response 200; driven with `curl --http2-prior-knowledge`):
- **(a) Stats namespace:** `http.ingress_http.fault.aborts_injected: 4` after 4 aborts — MATCHES SPEC §2.1 `http.<hcm_stat_prefix>.fault.aborts_injected` (value-exact). Upstream also emits 4 sibling fault counters (`active_faults`/`delays_injected`/`faults_overflow`/`response_rl_injected`) at 0 — the deferred surfaces per SPEC §4; phase 11 registers only `aborts_injected`.
- **(b) Abort body:** `"fault filter abort"` = 18 bytes, hex `66 61 75 6c 74 20 66 69 6c 74 65 72 20 61 62 6f 72 74` — MATCHES SPEC §2.2 projection exactly. **No off-by-one** (unlike the phase-10 RBAC 1-byte correction that the SPEC §7 signpost warned was "the most likely ADR-0035 trigger"). Production shape `Bytes::from_static(b"fault filter abort")` per PLAN lock-in #16.
- **(c) H2 abort header set:** `{server, content-length, content-type, date}`, NO `connection` — MATCHES SPEC §2.2 + D6 projection exactly. Confirms the `decorate_filter_synth_response_h2` target set (content-length always + server/date/content-type only-if-missing; no connection).

**Disposition:** all 3 matched → **no inline ADR-0035; no SPEC.md edit; DECISIONS.md ledger head stays at ADR-0034** (recommended SPEC §7 option A posture). The other 3 conditional ADR-0035 slots (B fractional-deferral durability; C foundations grant; D `Driver::Http2ProbeList` durability) all DEFER per recommended posture. Next available: **ADR-0035**.

**Split-gate verdict (PLAN §3):** single-phase, no split. PLAN materializes **8 tasks / ~1520 LoC** (production ~480, tests ~745, fixture/harness/doc ~295) — marginally at the soft ~1500-LoC gate (+1.3%; same posture as phase-10's ~1525). Release valve if state-3 drifts past ~1600: D8.1 option (b) trim (single-probe `Driver::Http2` abort + backstop pass-through) per PLAN lock-in #38. No nest-split per parent-08 SPEC §6.1 alternative (vi).

**7 PLAN-write SPEC corrections (PLAN §1):** (1) `error.rs:51`/`:57` is NOT a reject list — it is a `RouterNotTerminal` Display test fixture; the fault name is rejected by serde tag-matching (`HttpFilterTypedConfig` `#[serde(tag = "@type", deny_unknown_fields)]`), so **D5 does NOT touch `error.rs`** (adding the variant + validator arm is the entire rejected→supported move). (2) `ConfigError` lives in `lib.rs` (the 3 fault variants land there). (3) `HeaderMatcher::matches(&[(String,String)])` re-confirmed at `matcher.rs:19`. (4) The 3-arg `build_from_config`/`build` (`filters, registry, hcm_stat_prefix`) already exists (phase 10) — **NO signature widening this phase** (unlike phase 10, which widened the H1 HCM call site). (5) `FilterResponse.reason: Option<&'static str>` (abort uses `reason: None`). (6) H2 writer-arm sites re-confirmed: decode `SynthFromDecode(r)` at `hcm.rs:373`, encode `StopAndSend(replacement)` at `:436`, `build_http_response` at `response.rs:29` strips `H2_FORBIDDEN_HOP_BY_HOP` (contains `"connection"`). (7) All 3 §6.2 projections match → no ADR/SPEC edit.
